We propose bounded-fidelity sim-as-demo-stage, a design pattern that suppresses contact physics within explicitly bracketed handoff envelopes while preserving full dynamics elsewhere. The construction uses MuJoCo's mocap-body primitive driven by a 220-line Python adapter that the governance bridge invokes via structured intents. We formalise audit-chain stability as byte-equality of the hashed event log across replays and identify two structural envelope properties that imply it. Across N=1000 replays per posture, the mocap variant produces one distinct audit-chain hash (1000/1000 byte-identical; Wilson 95% CI [0.997, 1.000]); the contact-force baseline produces 584 distinct hashes (993/1000 diverged; CI [0.987, 0.998]). A timestep sweep (1, 2, 5, 10 ms) shows the divergence is structural, not a tuning artefact: it stays at 0.985 at every timestep. Envelope-edge timing jitter (+/-10 simulation steps, 1,400 replays) produces 0 divergence, and audit chains remain byte-equal across K in {1, 2, 3} sequentially handed-off objects (1,500 replays) with sub-linear per-pick-and-place overhead. The pattern gives benchmark designers audit-chain reproducibility at near-zero engineering cost; we also map where it is harmful (sim-to-real validation, policy training, contact-rich tasks) so it is not mis-deployed.

</details>


### [176] [Multi-Reference Path Tracking Control for an Agricultural Tractor with Nonlinear Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00057)
*Marcel Moll,Timo Oksanen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出一种基于非线性模型预测控制（NMPC）的拖拉机路径跟踪控制器，将多段分段线性参考路径直接纳入目标函数，并在田间试验中验证，平均求解时间3.45 ms，横向跟踪误差均值6.1 cm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引导拖拉机沿预定参考路径行驶是精准农业的关键环节，需要高精度、实时的路径跟踪控制方法。

Method: 开发基于非线性模型预测控制的路径跟踪控制器，将分段线性参考路径的多段直接嵌入目标函数；同时提出从完整路径中选取可行参考段的方法；通过Tractor Implement Management转向接口控制拖拉机进行田间试验评估。

Result: NMPC求解器平均3.45 ms收敛，在曲线参考路径上实现平均绝对横向误差6.1 cm的跟踪精度。

Conclusion: 该NMPC路径跟踪控制器具备良好的实时性与跟踪精度，适用于精准农业中的拖拉机自动导航。

Abstract: Guiding a tractor along a predefined reference path is a key component of precision agriculture. This study develops a path tracking controller based on Nonlinear Model Predictive Control, which incorporates multiple segments of a piecewise-linear reference path directly into the objective function. In addition, methods for selecting viable reference segments from the full path are presented. The control system is evaluated during a field test with a tractor controlled via the Tractor Implement Management steering interface. The NMPC solver converged on average after 3.45 ms and tracked the curved reference path with a mean absolute cross-track error of 6.1 cm.

</details>


### [177] [Probabilistic Plan Legibility with Off-the-shelf Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00065)
*Michele Persiani,Thomas Hellström*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种适用于任意PDDL领域的可读性规划方法，通过将可读性研究扩展到经典规划，并利用二阶心智理论估计观察者视角，实现无需专门规划器的可读计划生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可读性规划旨在让计划从观察者视角能最大程度区分其目标与其他候选目标。此前研究多限于特定场景，本文希望将其扩展到经典规划中任意PDDL领域，并应用于人-机器人协作等场景，使自主机器人通过生成可读计划隐式传达目标。

Method: 提出一种针对任意PDDL领域的可读性规划方法，将先前可读性研究扩展到经典规划，无需构建专门规划器；并通过连接规划者与观察者任务空间的二阶心智理论来估计观察者视角。

Result: 在多个PDDL规划领域进行了基准测试。结果总体上显示计划可读性与计划效率之间存在权衡，但并非所有规划领域都能以相同方式提升可读性，因此需要一个正则化因子来平衡可读性与效率。

Conclusion: 可读性规划可扩展到任意PDDL经典规划领域，并可应用于人-机器人协作场景；但可读性与效率之间的权衡因领域而异，需要引入正则化因子进行平衡。

Abstract: Legible planning is the creation of plans that best disambiguate their goals from a set of other candidates from an observer's perspective. In this paper we propose a method for legible planning for arbitrary PDDL domains, by extending previous research on legibility to classical planning without requiring to construct ad-hoc planners. We also discuss how the observer perspective may be estimated through a second order theory of mind that connects the planner's and the observer's task spaces. Our solution can for example be deployed in human-robot teaming scenarios, where an autonomous robot in a team can implicitly communicate its goal by producing legible plans. We present benchmark results on several PDDL planning domains. Our results generally show that plan legibility is a trade-off with plan efficiency, however, not all planning domains allows to increase legibility in the same way and a regularizing factor to balance legibility and efficiency was proved necessary.

</details>


### [178] [HumanoidTTT: Test-Time Capability Reuse for Efficient Humanoid Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00198)
*Jingtai Yang,Yining Wu,Yanjun Li,Zeyu Zhang,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 HumanoidTTT 框架，用于连续人形控制中的测试时能力复用：通过选择式全运动复用（仅从认证可用入口状态直接复用已验证的完整运动）与测试时能力整合（以部署复用反馈决定哪些合格能力保留在有限的全运动存储中），实现零不安全接受、16.4×端到端加速，并在线整合比冻结版本每200次请求多避免13.2次生成器调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人在连续部署中会反复请求相同或相似的运动能力，但中间执行的其他运动可能改变机器人进入状态，使过去成功的运动无法盲目重放；同时部署过程中不断积累已验证能力，而存储有限，必须判断哪些能力值得保留。因此需要一种可靠的测试时复用机制。

Method: 提出 HumanoidTTT 框架，包含两个核心组件：1）选择式全运动复用（Selective Full-Motion Reuse），仅从认证为可用的入口状态授权直接复用已验证的完整运动，被接受的复用可绕过重新生成；2）测试时能力整合（Test-Time Capability Consolidation），利用后续部署中的复用情况作为反馈，在有界全运动存储（Full-Motion Store）中自适应决定哪些合格能力得以持久保留。

Result: 实验中实现了零不安全接受，端到端相较重新生成取得16.4×加速；在线整合相比冻结版本，在每200次请求中额外避免13.2次生成器调用。

Conclusion: HumanoidTTT 能够在连续部署中可靠且高效地复用已验证的运动能力，并自适应地保留有用的能力。代码与网站已公开。

Abstract: Recent advances in motion generation and whole-body tracking have enabled humanoid robots to execute increasingly diverse motions, yet the same motion capabilities may be requested repeatedly during continual deployment. Reliable reuse is challenging because intervening motions can change the robot's entry state, making previously successful motions unsafe to replay blindly. Meanwhile, validated capabilities accumulate during deployment, while bounded storage requires deciding which ones are worth retaining. To address these challenges, we present HumanoidTTT, a framework for test-time capability reuse in continual humanoid control. Specifically, we introduce Selective Full-Motion Reuse, which authorizes direct reuse of validated complete motions only from certified applicable entry states, allowing accepted reuse to bypass fresh generation. We further introduce Test-Time Capability Consolidation, which adapts which qualified capabilities persist in a bounded Full-Motion Store using subsequent deployment reuse as feedback. Experiments demonstrate zero unsafe accepts and a 16.4$\times$ end-to-end speedup over fresh generation, while online consolidation improves avoided generator calls by 13.2 per 200 requests over its frozen counterpart. Overall, HumanoidTTT enables reliable and efficient reuse of validated motion capabilities while adaptively retaining useful capabilities throughout continual deployment. Code: https://github.com/AIGeeksGroup/HumanoidTTT. Website: https://aigeeksgroup.github.io/HumanoidTTT.

</details>


### [179] [Real-Time Whole-Body Safe Motion Generation for Multi-Segment Tendon-Driven Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00220)
*Fangju Yang,Siyi Ma,Tonghao Guan,Tingcong Liu,Hang Yang,Zhengqiang Zhang,Jian S. Dai,Ke Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一个统一的驱动空间框架，用于平面多段肌腱驱动连续体机器人的实时运动生成，结合能量变曲率模型与多点CBF-QP实现全身避障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肌腱驱动连续体机器人的实时运动生成需要准确建模非均匀弯曲以及实现全身碰撞避免，现有方法在这两方面存在不足。

Method: 采用基于能量的变曲率模型，捕捉空间变化的肌腱间距和弯曲刚度，并提供解析雅可比用于微分逆运动学和安全性监测；同时使用多点CBF-QP在障碍物运动与驱动速度约束下保证主干间隙，其决策维度仅取决于独立驱动段的数量。

Result: 模型与GVS参考高度一致，最大曲率误差为5.223×10⁻² m⁻¹；在100次MuJoCo试验中，静态和动态场景下无碰撞成功率分别为96%和100%，而无CBF约束时约为60%和80%；穿孔测试验证了受限环境中的安全运动；在600个主干监测点下，平均控制步时间为6.66 ms。

Conclusion: 该框架能够以实时性能实现全身安全运动生成，验证了所提方法在连续体机器人避障与运动生成方面的有效性。

Abstract: Real-time motion generation for tendon-driven continuum robots requires accurate modeling of nonuniform bending and whole-body collision avoidance. This paper presents a unified actuation-space framework for planar multi-segment tendon-driven continuum robots. An energy-based variable-curvature model captures spatially varying tendon spacing and bending stiffness and provides analytical Jacobians for differential inverse kinematics and safety monitoring. A multipoint CBF-QP enforces backbone clearance under obstacle motion and actuation-velocity bounds, while its decision dimension depends only on the number of independently actuated segments. The model closely agrees with GVS references, with a maximum curvature error of $5.223 \times 10^{-2}\,\mathrm{m}^{-1}$. Over 100 MuJoCo trials, the proposed method achieves collision-free success rates of 96% and 100% in static and dynamic scenarios, respectively, compared with approximately 60% and 80% without CBF constraints. Hole-traversal tests further demonstrate safe motion in constrained environments. With 600 backbone monitoring points, the mean control-step time is 6.66 ms, demonstrating real-time whole-body safe motion generation.

</details>


### [180] [DriftOPD: Sequence-Level Reverse-KL Distillation for One-Step VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00317)
*Youngjun Jun,Kyumin Choi,Youngmin Kim,Seonghyun Jin,Sunwoo Park,Jangho Park,Jong Chul Ye*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 DriftOPD，一种无需教师、无需在线 rollout 的序列级 on-policy 蒸馏框架，将序列级反向 KL 分解为 chunk 级反向 KL 项与未来势能项，仅用离线数据和单步动作生成即可为连续 VLA 动作专家实现长时程优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型的动作专家常在滚动时域控制下生成短动作 chunk，chunk 级训练虽便于跨具身迁移，但只优化局部动作似然，未显式考虑长时程任务成功率。序列级强化学习虽能弥补这一缺陷，却需要策略 rollout 与闭环交互，对真实机器人操作而言代价高昂。

Method: 作者证明序列级反向 KL 散度可分解为 chunk 级反向 KL 项与刻画当前动作长时程影响的未来势能项。DriftOPD 分别用一步 drifting 目标优化前者、用从离线示范学得的 Q 函数 critic 优化后者，从而仅依赖离线数据和一步动作生成实现序列级优化，无需在线交互也无需独立教师模型。

Result: 在仿真与真实世界操作任务中，跨多种 VLA 架构，DriftOPD 总体优于现有一阶蒸馏基线，并达到与多步教师策略相当的任务成功率。

Conclusion: 结果表明，无需在线交互或单独教师，即可将长时程行为有效蒸馏进单步 VLA 动作专家，说明序列级长时程优化可借助离线数据与单步生成实现。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models increasingly rely on action experts that generate short action chunks under receding-horizon control. While chunk-level training is convenient across robot embodiments, it optimizes local action likelihood without explicitly accounting for long-horizon task success. Sequence-level reinforcement learning can address this limitation, but typically requires policy rollouts and closed-loop interaction, which are costly for real-robot manipulation. We introduce DriftOPD, a teacher-free, rollout-free framework for sequence-level on-policy distillation of continuous VLA action experts. We show that the sequence-level reverse Kullback-Leibler (KL) divergence decomposes into a chunk-level reverse-KL term and a future-potential term that captures the long-horizon effect of the current action. DriftOPD optimizes these two terms using a one-step drifting objective and a Q-function critic learned from offline demonstrations, respectively, enabling sequence-level optimization with only offline data and one-step action generation. Across multiple VLA architectures in simulation and real-world manipulation, DriftOPD generally outperforms existing one-step distillation baselines while achieving task success performance comparable to multi-step teacher policies. These results demonstrate that long-horizon behavior can be effectively distilled into one-step VLA action experts without online interaction or a separate teacher.

</details>


### [181] [Retrospective Open-Vocabulary Memory for Long-Term Object Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00330)
*Jiaming Wang,Zhiwei Xue,Chen Jizhuo,Peng Shiqi,Harold Soh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出回顾式开放词汇记忆的概率推断框架，核心思想是按"观测机会"来加权证据，推出ECROM方法，并建立长期基准进行评测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期物体搜索需要从反复但不均匀的环境观测中学习物体的常见出现位置。现有方法难以处理观测机会不均带来的偏差，且需要支持仅查询时才指定的开放词汇概念。

Method: 将回顾式开放词汇记忆形式化为对"截断观测（censored observations）"的概率推断，核心是按观测机会比例权衡证据：一次检测或未检测只应基于机器人对该位置的实际观测机会来影响信念。基于此提出ECROM，用于估计仅在查询时指定的概念的长期流行度，并将所得信念直接转化为主动搜索先验。同时构建了十套HM3D住宅的受控长期基准，独立地变化物体摆放与重复遍历中的观测机会。

Result: ECROM在留出查询上使support-level AP提升4.5个点，搜索SPL提升4.2个点，均优于各项指标中最强的对比记忆方法。

Conclusion: 按观测机会加权的概率推断能有效建模长期开放词汇记忆并改进主动搜索；基准、数据集与代码将开源。

Abstract: Long-term object search requires learning where objects usually appear from repeated but uneven observations of a changing environment. We formulate retrospective open-vocabulary memory as probabilistic inference from censored observations, where the key idea is to reason with evidence per opportunity: a detection or non-detection should influence belief only in proportion to the robot's opportunity to observe the corresponding location. We introduce ECROM, which uses this principle to estimate long-term prevalence for concepts specified only at query time and converts the resulting belief directly into an active-search prior. To evaluate this problem, we introduce a controlled long-term benchmark in ten HM3D homes that independently varies object placement and observation opportunity across repeated traversals. ECROM improves support-level AP on held-out queries by 4.5 points and search SPL by 4.2 points over the strongest competing memory in each metric. The benchmark, dataset, and code will be open-sourced.

</details>


### [182] [IndoorBEV: A Lightweight Real-Time LiDAR BEV Perception System for Indoor Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00355)
*Haichuan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IndoorBEV是一个轻量级室内LiDAR感知框架，通过高度感知BEV表示与几何条件特征融合，在微小模型规模下实现低延迟、低内存的语义与目标检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内LiDAR感知需在严格延迟与内存约束下理解杂乱三维环境。点云/体素方法计算开销大，而传统BEV虽高效却丢失垂直几何信息。本文旨在缓解这一效率与三维信息间的权衡。

Method: 提出高度感知BEV表示：用统计高度特征与多频高度编码总结每个BEV单元的垂直点分布，使三维线索由二维卷积高效处理。轻量编码器融合几何特征、多尺度局部表示和紧凑全局场景上下文。解耦密集预测头联合输出语义BEV图与有向目标边界框。

Result: 模型仅0.6M参数、2.3MB存储；在NVIDIA AGX Orin上每次推理占用21.52MB GPU内存，平均延迟169.6ms（200ms截止期），超时率1.8%。在仿真、真实机器人扫描及开源室内点云数据集上均取得精度、延迟与内存的良好权衡。

Conclusion: 在紧凑BEV表示中显式编码垂直几何，是资源高效室内LiDAR感知的有效途径。

Abstract: Efficient indoor LiDAR perception is challenging because mobile robots must understand cluttered three-dimensional environments under strict latency and memory constraints. Existing point-based and voxel-based methods often incur substantial computational overhead, whereas conventional bird's-eye-view (BEV) representations improve efficiency at the cost of discarding vertical geometric information. We present IndoorBEV, a lightweight LiDAR perception framework that mitigates this tradeoff through a height-aware BEV representation and geometry-conditioned feature fusion. IndoorBEV summarizes the vertical point distribution in each BEV cell using statistical height features and multi-frequency height encoding, allowing informative three-dimensional cues to be processed efficiently by two-dimensional convolutions. A lightweight encoder then integrates complementary geometric features with multi-scale local representations and compact global scene context. Decoupled dense prediction heads jointly produce semantic BEV maps and oriented object bounding boxes. IndoorBEV contains only 0.6M parameters and requires 2.3 MB of model storage. On an NVIDIA AGX Orin, it uses 21.52 MB of GPU memory per inference and achieves a mean latency of 169.6 ms under a 200 ms perception deadline, with a deadline miss ratio of 1.8\%. Evaluations on simulated scenes, real-world robot scans, and an open-source indoor point-cloud dataset demonstrate a favorable tradeoff among perception accuracy, latency, and memory consumption. These results indicate that explicitly encoding vertical geometry within a compact BEV representation provides an effective approach to resource-efficient indoor LiDAR perception.

</details>


### [183] [DexPolicy: Scheduled Exploration for Trajectory-Guided Dexterous Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00360)
*Haoyu Wang,Siyuan Qian,Yanjun Li,Zeyu Zhang,Yandong Guo,Boxin Shi,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DexPolicy，将强化学习的探索尺度设为训练步数的显式退火函数，从宽探索逐步收窄，在PPO、GRPO、FPO三种策略优化设定下显著提升灵巧操作的目标成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧操作强化学习需先发现手指-物体接触再精确控制物体，但服务于探索的动作噪声会干扰精确控制。在ViViDex等轨迹引导设定中，基线PPO训练5M步后仍停留在初始动作噪声水平，说明需要显式控制探索尺度。

Method: DexPolicy把探索尺度设为训练步数的显式函数，从宽到窄退火，同时固定损失、网络结构、奖励与优化器设置。研究三种策略优化设定：PPO、无critic的GRPO续训、流参数化的PPO变体（FPO）。

Result: 五个YCB物体、三个随机种子上，确定性Target平均成功率：FPO由49.4%升至68.1%，GRPO由14.1%升至45.4%，PPO由32.0%升至35.7%。在RealMan RM75机械臂配Inspire/RH56手上，三个物体共360次试验：FPO由25.0%升至85.0%，GRPO由10.0%升至63.3%，PPO由8.3%升至43.3%。PPO组件筛选显示噪声控制优于所测试的优化器收缩；所选PPO调度在三个测试物体上优于端点相同的线性衰减。

Conclusion: 训练回报、确定性Target成功率与对执行噪声的容忍度相互解耦，因此调度应按预期执行条件下的最终任务成功率来评判，且需分任务、分策略优化设定评估。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for dexterous manipulation must discover finger-object contacts and then control the object precisely; the action noise that serves the first goal can interfere with the second. In trajectory-guided settings such as ViViDex, where RL refine hand-object trajectories from human video, our baseline PPO runs end near their initial action noise after 5M steps, motivating explicit control of exploration scale. DexPolicy makes that scale an explicit function of training steps, annealing from broad to narrow exploration while holding loss, architecture, reward, and optimizer settings fixed. We study three policy-optimization settings: PPO, critic-free GRPO continuation, and a flow-parameterized PPO variant (FPO). Across five YCB objects and three training seeds, mean deterministic Target success rises from 49.4% to 68.1% (FPO), 14.1% to 45.4% (GRPO), and 32.0% to 35.7% (PPO). On a RealMan RM75 arm with an Inspire/RH56 hand, 360 trials over three objects raise mean Target success from 25.0% to 85.0% (FPO), 10.0% to 63.3% (GRPO), and 8.3% to 43.3% (PPO), with one trained model per object-method condition. PPO component screening favors noise control over the tested optimizer contraction; the selected PPO schedule yields higher mean Target success than linear decay with the same endpoints on three tested objects. Training return, deterministic Target success, and tolerance to execution noise dissociate; schedules should therefore be judged by terminal task success under the intended execution conditions, per task and policy-optimization setting. Code: https://github.com/AIGeeksGroup/DexPolicy. Website: https://aigeeksgroup.github.io/DexPolicy.

</details>


### [184] [DeepJEPA: Scaling World Models from Within](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00368)
*Zijian Jin,Yunbei Zhang,Yuanzhe Liu,Ming Liu,Baian Chen,Weirui Ye,Shilong Liu,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DeepJEPA将世界模型中的过渡深度视为内层测试时缩放轴，通过权重绑定和学习的自适应迭代更新来实现按需深度计算，在五个视觉控制任务中提升或匹配最强固定深度规划器，平均仅需1.00-1.26次更新/过渡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型规划器通常通过滚动更远、采样更多轨迹或优化更久来扩展计算，且对每个想象过渡分配相同计算量。作者指出这种均匀加深的方式浪费计算并可能降低规划性能，因为有益的细化仅集中在少数决策关键事件上。

Method: 提出DeepJEPA：一种权重绑定的联合嵌入预测世界模型，把过渡深度作为内层测试时缩放轴，学习对每个候选和推演步判断是否值得再执行一次循环更新。

Result: 在五种视觉控制设置中，DeepJEPA在不劣于甚至优于最强固定深度规划器的同时，平均每个过渡仅需1.00-1.26次更新；额外计算集中出现在接触起始和持续物体交互阶段。表示探测进一步表明，规划性能提升并不需要统一更优的物体状态可解码性。

Conclusion: DeepJEPA将世界模型缩放重新定义为：在能改变规划器决策的地方分配内部计算——在决策关键过渡处思考更深，而非让每次推演都均匀更深或更长。

Abstract: World-model planners typically scale outward by rolling farther, sampling more trajectories, or optimizing longer, while assigning the same computation to every imagined transition. We show that making every transition uniformly deeper wastes computation and can degrade planning because useful refinement is concentrated at a small set of decision-critical events. We introduce DeepJEPA, a weight-tied joint-embedding predictive world model that treats transition depth as an inner test-time scaling axis and learns when another recurrent update is worth computing for each candidate and rollout step. Across five visual-control settings, DeepJEPA improves or matches the strongest fixed-depth planner while averaging only 1.00-1.26 updates per transition. Its additional computation concentrates at contact onset and sustained object interaction, where latent corrections can change which candidates enter the planner's elite set and which action is selected. Representation probes further show that improved planning does not require uniformly better object-state decodability. DeepJEPA therefore reframes world-model scaling as a problem of allocating internal computation where it can change the planner's decision: think deeper at decision-critical transitions instead of making every rollout uniformly deeper or longer.

</details>


### [185] [Whole-Body Aerial Grasping and Lifting via Partial Visual Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00404)
*Jiaye Jin,Rui Jin,Xinhang Xu,Haotian Jin,Ruiyang Liu,Yi Wang,Jiayan Zhao,Kun Cao,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出循环式教师-学生框架，在仿真中学习单一策略，联合控制飞行、机械臂运动与夹爪闭合，无需显式任务阶段输入，实现空中抓取-举升任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空中抓取-举升需要全身协调，涉及接近、抓取、举升阶段，且目标观测部分可见。早期接近失败会减少后续阶段训练暴露，可见性变化又使对齐与闭合时机难以把控，因此需要解决阶段衔接与时机控制问题。

Method: 采用特权教师通过强化学习训练，配合关键状态课程，先暴露抓取与举升状态再连接到正常接近轨迹；其行为蒸馏到循环视觉学生，用双视角点云与本体感受替代特权目标状态，并整合观测历史进行闭环控制；另设闭合目标，基于持续模型定义的准备序列监督闭合时机。仿真中用条件触发锁存、虚拟附着与基于力旋量的载荷加载模型，完成短距离举升。

Result: 在8,996个完成仿真回合中，冻结学生在标称、物理/控制随机化以及额外相机随机化条件下，全任务成功率分别为99.97%、97.14%、95.84%。标称条件下按锁存数加权的各随机种子抓取对齐误差90百分位均值为8.12 mm。

Conclusion: 结果表明，该循环教师-学生框架无需任务阶段输入即可在仿真中高效学习空中抓取-举升的全身协调策略，并在多种随机化下保持高成功率与对齐精度。

Abstract: Aerial grasp-and-lift tasks require whole-body coordination across approach, acquisition, and lifting under partial target observations. Early approach failures can limit exposure to later task stages during training, while changing visibility complicates alignment and closure timing during execution. We present a recurrent teacher-student framework that learns a single policy in simulation to jointly command flight, arm motion, and gripper closure without an explicit task-phase input. A privileged teacher learns through reinforcement learning with a critical-state curriculum that exposes acquisition and lifting states before connecting them to normal approach trajectories. Its behavior is distilled into a recurrent visual student that replaces privileged target states with dual-view point clouds and proprioception, integrating observation history for closed-loop control. A dedicated closure objective supervises closure timing from sustained model-defined readiness sequences. Training and primary evaluation use a simulated acquisition-and-payload model with condition-triggered latching, virtual attachment, and wrench-based payload loading for short-distance lifting. Across 8,996 completed simulation episodes under this model, the frozen student achieves full-task success rates of 99.97%, 97.14%, and 95.84% under nominal, physics/control-randomized, and additional camera-randomized conditions, respectively. The nominal latch-count-weighted mean of per-seed 90th-percentile alignment errors at acquisition is 8.12 mm.

</details>


### [186] [Towards a General Humanoid Loco-Manipulation Model via Egocentric Whole-Body Human Data Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00438)
*Chongyang Xu,Zhao Wu,Jin Chen,Yiming Jiang,Jinhui Ye,Yuming Jiang,Shifeng Zhang,Ziliang Feng,Mu Xu,Yilun Chen,Li Lu,Steven C. H. Hoi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 HumanVerse-500 数据集与 λ₀ 全身人形视觉-语言-动作策略，通过三阶段训练利用人类经验实现可扩展的人形移动-操作学习，在 SIMPLE 及 4 项真实任务上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形全身操作虽进步迅速，但自我中心人类视频对全身运动及手-物交互协同的监督覆盖有限，而通过人形遥操作获取此类监督成本高、难以规模化。因此作者探索如何利用人类经验支持可扩展的人形移动-操作学习。

Method: 构建 HumanVerse-500（500 小时开放环境中多样化人类移动-操作行为数据集，用轻量可穿戴系统同步自我中心视频与身体、手部运动）；并基于此开发 λ₀ 全身人形 VLA 策略，采用三阶段训练：先学多样化自我中心数据集的交互，再用 HumanVerse-500 协调身体与手部运动，最后适配下游任务与机器人本体。各阶段学习共享表征空间以迁移人类经验，域特定接口处理人机状态与动作差异。

Result: 在 SIMPLE 及 4 项真实世界移动-操作任务上评估 λ₀，取得当前最优性能；并进一步分析其规模化行为、泛化能力及各训练阶段贡献。

Conclusion: 人类数据可有效支撑下游全身人形控制，所提数据集、代码与模型将开源以支持后续研究。

Abstract: Humanoid whole-body manipulation has advanced rapidly, enabling policies to coordinate locomotion, posture, bimanual interaction, and dexterous hand movements. Meanwhile, egocentric human videos provide diverse examples of everyday interactions across objects and scenes, offering scalable supervision without robot operation. However, existing supervision from these videos provides limited coverage of whole-body movement and coordination with hand-object interaction, while obtaining such supervision through humanoid teleoperation is also costly and difficult to scale. We therefore explore how human experience can support scalable learning of humanoid loco-manipulation. To support this study, we introduce HumanVerse-500, a 500-hour dataset of diverse human loco-manipulation behaviors in open-world environments, collected with a lightweight wearable system that synchronizes egocentric video with body and hand motion. Building on this dataset, we develop $λ_0$, a whole-body humanoid vision-language-action policy, through three-stage training that first learns interaction from diverse egocentric datasets, then coordinates body and hand motion using HumanVerse-500, and finally adapts the policy to downstream tasks and robot embodiments. Across these stages, $λ_0$ learns a shared representation space for human experience transfer, while domain-specific interfaces handle differences between human and robot states and actions. We evaluate $λ_0$ on SIMPLE and 4 real-world loco-manipulation tasks, achieving state-of-the-art performance, and further analyze its scaling behavior, generalization, and training-stage contributions to understand how human data support downstream whole-body humanoid control. We will release our code, models, and data to support further research.

</details>


### [187] [ScaffoldM3C: A Multimodal Sequential Monte Carlo Framework for Generative Stable Construction Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00487)
*Gadiel Sznaier Camps,Chengyang He,Guillaume Sartoretti,Eduardo Montijano,Mac Schwager*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ScaffoldM3C，一种多模态轻量自回归模型，将3D结构构建建模为下一块生成任务，结合脚手架token与顺序蒙特卡洛实现稳定拼装，比基线小4倍、推理快5-20倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主构建物理可实现的3D结构面临组合动作空间大、组件可互换、多解装配序列及严格稳定性要求等挑战。现有基于大语言模型微调的文本生成式方法存在不足：不支持多模态（文本、图像、草图）条件输入，忽视脚手架在稳定中间结构中的实际作用，且推理速度慢。

Method: 将构建形式化为具有多种潜在装配动作和多种任务条件模态的概率式下一块生成任务；引入辅助脚手架块token以显式利用脚手架稳定性。提出ScaffoldM3C——面向稳定积木构建的多模态轻量自回归模型，生成一组下一步候选块，并利用顺序蒙特卡洛（SMC）维护可能的装配序列种群，从而同时考虑多种装配方向。通过扩展StableText2Brick数据集加入图像条件提示和脚手架稳定构建序列进行训练。

Result: ScaffoldM3C比竞争基线小4倍，推理速度提升5至20倍，同时构建质量与最先进方法相当，整体稳定性更高。通过仿真和真实机器人装配演示验证了有效性。

Conclusion: ScaffoldM3C以轻量多模态自回归模型结合脚手架token与顺序蒙特卡洛，在保证构建质量的同时显著提升稳定性与推理效率，并在真实机器人装配中验证了实用价值。

Abstract: Autonomously constructing physically realizable 3D structures remains a significant challenge due to combinatorial action spaces, interchangeable components, equifinal assembly sequences, and strict stability requirements during construction. State-of-the-art methods fine-tune large language models for text-based generative construction. However, these approaches do not allow for Multimodal (text, image, sketch) conditioning, overlook the practical role of scaffolding for stabilizing intermediate structures, and suffer from slow inference speeds. Therefore, we formulate construction as a probabilistic next-block generation task with multiple potential assembly actions and multiple potential task conditioning modalities. Concurrently, we explicitly consider the utility of scaffolding by introducing an auxiliary scaffold block token. We present Scaffold Multimodal Monte Carlo (ScaffoldM3C), a multimodal, lightweight, auto-regressive model for stable block-based construction, that proposes a set of next-step candidate blocks. Leveraging these candidates, we utilize Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) to maintain a population of possible assembly sequences, allowing us to consider multiple, potentially different, assembly directions simultaneously. We train our multimodal architecture by extending the StableText2Brick dataset to contain image conditioning prompts and scaffold-stabilized build sequences. ScaffoldM3C is 4x smaller than competing baselines, yielding a 5x to 20x speedup during inference, while achieving comparable construction quality to state-of-the-art methods and higher overall stability. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach through simulations and real-world robot assembly demonstrations.

</details>


### [188] [Same Scene, Different Task: Skill Alignment for Compositional Generalization in VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00524)
*Taegeun Yang,Youngju Na,Yoonki Cho,Sung-Eui Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CRAFT方法，通过技能表示将已演示技能的动作监督迁移到反事实指令对，缓解VLA模型对视觉捷径的依赖，提升未见技能组合的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型难以泛化到微调演示中未出现的技能组合，即使每个组成技能都已被演示过。论文聚焦于一种失败模式——视觉捷径：微调时视觉观测可能充当指令的代理，导致策略依据相似观测执行已演示的组合，而非执行指令所指定的组合。

Method: 用反事实配对进行训练：固定某条演示的观测，将指令改为未演示过的技能组合。此类配对缺少对应的演示动作目标。论文指出所需技能已被演示，但因其动作随观测变化而不能直接作为目标。CRAFT利用可跨同一技能不同执行复用的技能表示，把已演示执行中的监督迁移到反事实配对上。

Result: 在三个VLA模型和两个仿真基准上，CRAFT提升了未演示组合的成功率，同时保持已演示组合的高成功率；在真实机器人上也提升了组合泛化能力。

Conclusion: 通过技能表示迁移监督来训练反事实配对，可有效缓解视觉捷径问题，提升VLA模型的组合泛化性能。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models often struggle to generalize to skill combinations absent from their fine-tuning demonstrations, even when every constituent skill has been demonstrated. We focus on a vision shortcut as one failure mode: during fine-tuning, visual observations can serve as a proxy for the instruction, so a policy may execute a demonstrated combination associated with similar observations rather than the instructed combination. This motivates training with counterfactual pairs formed by holding a demonstration observation fixed while changing the instruction to specify an undemonstrated combination. These pairs, however, lack corresponding demonstrated action targets. Crucially, the currently required skill has already been demonstrated, but actions from those executions cannot serve as direct targets because the same skill can require different actions across observations. We propose CRAFT, which transfers supervision from demonstrated executions of the required skill to counterfactual pairs using skill representations that can be reused across executions of the same skill. Across three VLA models and two simulation benchmarks, CRAFT improves success on undemonstrated combinations while maintaining high success on demonstrated ones; it also improves compositional generalization on a real robot. Project website: https://taegeunyang.github.io/craft/

</details>


### [189] [Does Continual Imitation Learning Remain Grounded? A Language-Perturbed Benchmark for Robotic Task Retention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00542)
*Siddeshwar Raghavan,Ziqin Yuan,Fengqing Zhu,Byung-Cheol Min*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个评测协议，研究在持续模仿学习过程中机器人语言引导行为如何变化，发现强持续学习性能并不总意味着可靠的语言接地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续模仿学习通常只关注任务性能是否保持，但保留任务性能并不能保证策略行为真正基于语言，因为策略可能依赖场景线索、物体关联或记忆的任务结构。因此需要研究语言引导行为在持续学习过程中如何变化。

Method: 为LIBERO的Goal、Spatial、Object和Long套件构建保持语义与改变语义的指令变体；策略实验聚焦LIBERO-Goal，在每轮持续学习后评估原始指令和改写指令；在原始假设下比较代表性持续模仿学习方法，并将任务能力与语言敏感性分离；提出诊断指标衡量语义鲁棒性、目标适应性和语言敏感性。

Result: 结果显示，强持续学习性能并不总能转化为可靠的语言接地；所提诊断指标有助于判断保留下来的技能是否仍被指令正确引导。

Conclusion: 所提出的诊断方法补充了标准的学习与遗忘指标，可用于评估持续模仿学习中的语言接地可靠性，相关材料见 https://sites.google.com/view/stillgrounded 。

Abstract: Continual imitation learning evaluates whether a robot can learn new knowledge without forgetting previously learned skills. However, retaining task performance does not ensure the behavior remains grounded in language because policies may rely on scene cues, object associations, or memorized task structure. We introduce a benchmark protocol to study how language-guided behavior changes as robotic policies learn successive tasks. We construct meaning-preserving and meaning-changing instruction variants for the Goal, Spatial, Object, and Long suites of LIBERO. Policy experiments focus on LIBERO-Goal, evaluating Original and Paraphrase instructions after each continual-learning stage. We compare representative continual imitation learning methods under their original assumptions while separating task competence from language sensitivity. The proposed diagnostics complement standard learning and forgetting metrics by measuring semantic robustness, goal adaptation, and language sensitivity. Results show that strong continual-learning performance does not always translate to reliable language grounding, and our diagnostics help determine whether retained skills remain correctly guided by their instructions. Additional materials are available at https://sites.google.com/view/stillgrounded

</details>


### [190] [Token-World: World Modeling in Vision-Language Model Token Space for Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00575)
*Chuyao Fu,Xiaowei Chi,Yuhan Rui,Yu-kai Wang,Zezhong Qian,Xiaojie Zhang,Yunfan Lou,Kevin Zhang,Kuangzhi Ge,Chak Wing Mak,Zhiyang Chen,Athena Zhuoming Zhong,Hongyang Chen,Haoran Li,Yike Guo,Sirui Han,Shanghang Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Token-World 是一种动作条件世界模型，将 VLM 视觉 token 压缩为紧凑状态并在该空间建模未来动态，从而提升仿真保真度、策略一致性与闭环评估相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 世界模型仿真通常先预测未来 RGB 观测再重新编码为策略输入，仿真与下游策略执行之间存在间接接口，效率与一致性受限。作者希望探索能否在由 VLM 视觉 token 导出的紧凑、面向策略的状态中直接建模世界动态。核心难点是原始 VLM 视觉 token 维度高，自回归动态建模难以兼顾效率与准确性。

Method: 提出 Token-World：动作条件世界模型。将 VLM 特征压缩为紧凑 token 状态，在该降维空间学习未来动态，并将预测状态映射回原始面向策略的表示供下游使用。通过消融研究紧凑表示设计及维度对预测的影响。

Result: 在操作类基准上，Token-World 相比近期世界模型仿真器提升了开环特征保真度与策略-动作一致性，长时域 rollout 退化更慢；闭环评估中其仿真策略性能与参考策略性能的相关性显著更高（r=0.794 vs. Ctrl-World 的 0.583），同时仿真延迟更低。消融显示紧凑表示设计与维度对预测有实质性影响。

Conclusion: 在紧凑的、面向策略的 VLM token 状态空间中建模世界动态是可行且有效的，可带来更高保真度、更强闭环相关性与更低延迟，为 VLA 世界模型仿真提供了替代 RGB 预测-重编码范式的思路。

Abstract: A common approach to world-model simulation for vision-language-action (VLA) systems is to predict future RGB observations and then re-encode them into policy inputs, introducing an indirect interface between simulation and downstream policy execution. We instead investigate whether world dynamics can be modeled in a compact, policy-oriented state derived from VLM visual tokens. A key challenge is that raw VLM visual tokens are high-dimensional, making efficient and accurate autoregressive dynamics modeling challenging. To address this, we introduce Token-World, an action-conditioned world model that compresses VLM features into a compact token state, learns future dynamics in this reduced space, and maps predicted states back to the original policy-facing representation for downstream use. Across manipulation benchmarks, Token-World improves open-loop feature fidelity and policy-action consistency over recent world-model simulators, with slower degradation over long rollout horizons. In closed-loop evaluation, its simulated policy performance correlates more strongly with reference policy performance than Ctrl-World ($r=0.794$ vs.\ $0.583$), while requiring lower simulation latency. Ablations further show that compact-representation design and dimensionality substantially affect future-state prediction. Code will be available at https://chuyaofu.github.io/Token-World/.

</details>


### [191] [When Reasoning Helps Action: Monitoring and Steering Chain-of-Thought in Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00601)
*Sathwik Karnik,Joseph JR. Lee,Aryaman Gupta,Somil Bansal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出评估推理型VLA策略"可纠正性"与"可行动性"两个维度，并设计了TRUST方法（离线训练的价值模型，用于监测并引导CoT生成），在驾驶VLA上提升推理正确率与安全性，但在操作VLA上推理变好却未带来任务性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理型VLA策略会输出链式思考（CoT）轨迹，看似能解释并指导其动作，从而为运行时安全提供潜在接口（通过监测和纠正推理）。但尚不清楚这种接口何时能真正改善具身行为，因此作者要定义并操作化相应的评估标准。

Method: 定义两个评估轴：可纠正性（衡量不可靠推理能否在生成过程中被检测并改进）与可行动性（衡量推理纠正能否产生行为上有意义且方向正确的变化）。为实现可纠正性，提出TRUST（Token-level Reward for Utility-Steered Chain-of-Thought）：一个离线训练的价值模型，从部分前缀预测最终推理正确性，并据此监测、选择性地引导冻结VLA策略的推理生成。

Result: 在Alpamayo 1.5驾驶VLA上，TRUST监测正确性准确率88.9%，将推理正确率从75.9%提升到90.0%；在AlpaSim困难子集上，碰撞率相对未引导策略下降30.4%，最大轨迹误差下降11.5%，优于算力匹配的Best-of-4基线。在DeepThinkVLA操作VLA上，抓取状态断言正确率从69.3%升至90.2%，动作选择断言从68.8%升至85.9%，但LIBERO-Plus上的闭环任务性能基本不变。实证分析显示Alpamayo 1.5中行为效应与意图一致，而DeepThinkVLA中效应有限，解释了不同的任务级结果。

Conclusion: 推理正确性的提升并不自动意味着具身性能的提升，因此在将CoT用作运行时安全接口时，需要同时评估可纠正性与可行动性。

Abstract: Reasoning-enabled VLA policies expose chain-of-thought (CoT) traces that appear to explain and guide their actions, creating a potential interface for runtime safety through reasoning monitoring and correction. In this work, we define and operationalize two evaluation axes for assessing when this interface can improve embodied behavior: correctability, which measures whether unreliable reasoning can be detected and improved during generation, and actionability, which measures whether reasoning corrections produce behaviorally meaningful changes in the intended direction. To enable correctability, we introduce Token-level Reward for Utility-Steered Chain-of-Thought (TRUST), an offline-trained value model that predicts eventual reasoning correctness from partial prefixes and uses these estimates to monitor and selectively steer reasoning generation in frozen VLA policies. On the Alpamayo 1.5 driving VLA, TRUST monitors correctness with 88.9% accuracy and improves reasoning correctness from 75.9% to 90.0%. On a baseline-defined challenging subset in AlpaSim, TRUST reduces collision rate by 30.4% and maximum trajectory error by 11.5% relative to the unsteered policy, outperforming a compute-matched Best-of-4 baseline. On the DeepThinkVLA manipulation VLA, TRUST improves the correctness of grasp-state claims from 69.3% to 90.2% and action-choice claims from 68.8% to 85.9%, yet closed-loop task performance on LIBERO-Plus remains largely unchanged. Empirical analysis reveals intent-consistent behavioral effects in Alpamayo 1.5 but limited effects in DeepThinkVLA, helping interpret these different task-level outcomes. Together, our results show that gains in reasoning correctness do not automatically imply gains in embodied performance, motivating evaluation of correctability and actionability when using CoT as a runtime safety interface.

</details>


### [192] [TacDyn-WAM: Learning Implicit Tactile Dynamics in a Heterogeneous Visuo-Tactile World Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00638)
*Enyi Wang,Mingxin Wang,Quan Shi,Hetian Guo,Hongyu Wang,Xi Wang,Bin Qian,Yupeng Zheng,Wenxuan Song,Houde Liu,Yong Xu,Cheng Chi,Wenchao Ding,Yilun Chen,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TacDyn-WAM，一种异构视触觉世界动作模型，预测隐式触觉动态而非重建未来触觉观测，在UniVTAC和真机上取得领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有触觉世界动作模型多沿用视频生成管线，通过迭代去噪重建未来触觉观测；在部署漂移下，接触位置或力的微小变化会显著改变触觉像素，即使接触演化本身仍可预测，导致预测不可靠。

Method: TacDyn-WAM包含视觉专家与隐式触觉动态专家，分别在各自目标空间预测未来视觉与触觉表示，并通过联合注意力交互。学习TacRep：经掩码时空预测训练、由关系结构蒸馏正则化的动态感知触觉目标空间。触觉专家单次前向预测多时间尺度的未来表示及其变化，只读触觉记忆提供当前触觉状态。

Result: UniVTAC上仅用给定演示达81.5%平均成功率，达SOTA水平，与大规模视触觉轨迹预训练模型相当。消融验证两条触觉通路及TacRep优于像素重建与静态方案。五个真机任务平均71.0%，小规模预训练提升至85.0%。

Conclusion: 预测隐式触觉动态比重建触觉像素更鲁棒，双触觉通路与TacRep有效，方法兼具仿真与真机竞争力。

Abstract: World action models improve robotic manipulation by conditioning actions on predicted futures, yet existing tactile variants largely inherit video-generation pipelines that reconstruct future tactile observations through iterative denoising. Such prediction can become unreliable under deployment drift: small changes in contact position or force may substantially alter tactile pixels even when the underlying contact evolution remains predictable. We introduce TacDyn-WAM, a heterogeneous visuo-tactile world action model that predicts implicit tactile dynamics rather than reconstructing future tactile observations. It learns TacRep, a dynamics-aware tactile target space trained through masked spatio-temporal prediction on tactile clips and regularized by relational structure distillation. A visual expert and an Implicit Tactile Dynamics Expert predict future visual and tactile representations in separate target spaces while interacting through joint attention; the tactile expert predicts future representations and their changes at multiple horizons in a single forward pass, and a read-only tactile memory supplies the current tactile state. On UniVTAC, TacDyn-WAM achieves an average success rate of 81.5% using only the provided demonstrations, reaching state-of-the-art-level performance and remaining competitive with models pretrained on large-scale visuo-tactile trajectories. Ablations confirm the benefits of both tactile pathways and TacRep over pixel-reconstruction and static alternatives. On five real-robot tasks, TacDyn-WAM reaches 71.0% average success, and modest-scale pretraining raises it to 85.0%, further validating our method.

</details>


### [193] [Toward Humanoid Robots in Construction: A Teleoperation Feasibility Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00718)
*Parastoo Ali Pour,David R. Martin,Chang Min Hur,Bo Zhang,Tommy Zhou,Brandon Thomas Lichter,Shane Stanfield,Pramod Khargonekar,Mohammad Abdullah Al Faruque*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向Unitree G1人形机器人的遥操作系统，结合XR上半身控制与脚踏式移动，使单人操作者能同时进行操纵与移动，并在两项建筑任务上验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受持续劳动力短缺、危险工作环境以及人形机器人自主性不足的驱动，研究遥操作作为一种近期实用方案，以减轻工人身体负担并生成高质量示范数据。

Method: 构建基于XR的上半身控制与踏板式移动相结合的遥操作系统，由单一操作者控制Unitree G1人形机器人；从O*NET职业数据库中选取两项代表性建筑任务，与人工基线对比评估任务成功率与完成时间。

Result: 工具搬运任务成功率达100%，表面喷涂任务成功率为80%；但与人工执行相比，遥操作耗时明显更长。

Conclusion: 遥操作在建筑场景下具备可行性，可支持单人同时操纵与移动并完成一定任务，但在效率上仍显著落后于人工，未来需进一步优化以缩小时间差距。

Abstract: We present a teleoperation system that enables a single operator to perform construction tasks on a Unitree G1 humanoid, combining extended reality (XR) based upper body control with pedal-based locomotion to enable simultaneous manipulation and locomotion. Motivated by persistent labor shortages, hazardous working conditions, and challenges in humanoid autonomy, we investigate teleoperation as a practical near-term approach for reducing physical strain on workers while generating high quality demonstration data. We evaluate the system on two representative construction tasks drawn from O*NET occupational database, and report task success and completion time relative to a manual baseline. The system achieved 100% success on tool transport and 80% success on surface painting, with teleoperation requiring substantially more time compared to manual execution.

</details>


### [194] [CF-JEPA: Improving Robustness of JEPA World Models via Controllability Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00727)
*Morgan Byrd,Robert Wright,Sehoon Ha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CF-JEPA将JEPA世界模型的潜在空间分解为可控与不可控子空间，把干扰信息归入不可控区域，仅用控制相关潜信息完成任务，在2D/3D控制任务中表现相当，在干扰条件下唯一避免潜坍缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用视觉控制智能体需从无关背景中分离有用信息。JEPA式潜世界模型不做像素级重建，看似天然适合此任务，但仍对干扰信号敏感并出现潜坍缩。

Method: 提出Controllability Factorized JEPA（CF-JEPA），一种JEPA式世界模型，将潜空间划分为可控子空间与不可控子空间：干扰信息被捕获进不可控区域，控制相关潜信息用于下游任务。

Result: 在2D和3D控制任务中，标称条件下性能相当；在干扰条件下性能提升，且CF-JEPA是唯一不发生潜坍缩的模型。仿真机器人任务在干扰条件下的验证进一步体现其实用性。

Conclusion: 通过可控/不可控子空间分解，CF-JEPA在保持标称性能的同时显著提升干扰鲁棒性并避免潜坍缩，具备实际机器人应用潜力。

Abstract: Controlling an agent with vision requires being able to separate useful information from irrelevant background information. JEPA-style latent world models seem like a natural approach for this, as they do not perform pixel-level reconstruction; however, they are still sensitive to these distractor signals and experience latent collapse. In this work, we introduce Controllability Factorized JEPA (CF-JEPA), a JEPA-style world model which splits the latent space into controllable and uncontrollable subspaces. This factorization allows us to capture all the distractor information into the uncontrollable region, while we use the control-relevant latent information for our task. With this, we show comparable performance across 2D and 3D control tasks under nominal conditions and improved performance under distracted conditions, where CF-JEPA is the only model that does not experience latent collapse. We also validate our model under distracted conditions for a simulated robot task, highlighting the practical application of such a scheme.

</details>


### [195] [Measuring Asset and Scene Reconstruction Effects in Real-to-Sim Robot Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00731)
*Sanya Verma,Luca Cilio,Velissarios Christodoulou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过提升环境重建质量（度量尺度几何、人工物理参数、高保真场景），可将仿真与真实机器人策略评分的相关性从r=0.51提升到r=0.90，平均分误差降低10.56个百分点，从而缩小sim-to-real差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真评估因成本低、易重复而被广泛用于配合真实机器人策略评估，但其价值取决于仿真结果能否紧密跟踪真实机器人表现。因此作者希望检验：采用度量尺度物体几何、人工设定物理参数和场景重建的管线，是否能比默认开源方案更好地降低仿真与真实评分的分歧。

Method: 针对同一个双臂机器人单元构建两种仿真版本：一是"人工重建"（authored reconstruction），使用估计度量尺度的物体几何、投影纹理、人工物理参数及自建场景splat；二是"默认重建"（baseline），使用开源配方（单图生成网格、引擎默认物理、高斯splat场景）。两者使用相同的物体照片与场景视频。两个策略各运行五个任务，形成十个任务-策略对（cell），每个cell在每种重建中运行20次，其他设置固定。两种重建均与同一批真实试验对照，由第三方评估者打分。

Result: 十个cell的仿真与真实均值之间，Pearson相关系数为：人工重建r=0.90，默认重建r=0.51。平均得分误差为人工重建6.97个百分点，默认重建17.54个百分点，降低10.56个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过更高视觉保真度、人工物理参数和度量尺度来改进环境重建质量，可使仿真更贴近真实世界并缩小sim-to-real差距。作者公开了测试框架、逐次试验得分、每次报告运行的配置以及两种重建的资产与场景。

Abstract: Simulated evaluation is increasingly used alongside real-world evaluation of robot policies because it is cheaper and easier to repeat; however, its value depends on how closely its outcomes track the real robot's. We test whether our reconstruction pipeline, combining metrically scaled object geometry, authored physical parameters and scene reconstruction, reduces disagreement between simulated and real robot scores relative to a default open-source recipe. We constructed two simulated versions of one bimanual robot cell: an authored reconstruction, using object geometry at estimated metric scale, projected textures, authored physics and our own scene splat; and a baseline, referred to as the default reconstruction, using the open-source recipe of a generative single-image mesh, engine-default physics and a Gaussian-splat scene. Both reconstructions use the same object photographs and scene video. Two policies ran five tasks each, giving ten task-policy pairs, which we call cells; each cell was run twenty times in each reconstruction with all other settings held fixed. Both reconstructions were scored against the same real trials, graded by a third-party evaluator. Pearson correlation between the ten simulated and real cell means is r = 0.90 for the authored reconstruction and 0.51 for the default. Mean score error is 6.97 percentage points for the authored reconstruction and 17.54 for the default, a reduction of 10.56 percentage points. These results show that improving the quality of the environment reconstruction through higher visual fidelity, authored physics and metric scale makes the simulation more faithful to the real world and narrows the sim-to-real gap. We release the harness, the per-trial scores, every reported run's configuration, and the assets and scenes of both reconstructions.

</details>


### [196] [DITTO-X: Forward and Reverse Teleoperation for Dexterous Manipulation and Human Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00781)
*Zhanpeng He,Joaquin Palacios,Zhangyu Wang,Chenhao Li,Katelyn Lee,Matei Ciocarlie,C. Karen Liu,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DITTO-X 是一种手部无关的力反馈灵巧遥操作接口，能驱动多种商用灵巧手，并支持"反向遥操作"让操作者进入环路时已与机器人状态匹配，在数据采集与部署干预中均优于商用跟踪手套。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥操作演示是机器人操作数据的主要来源，遥操作干预也是部署时纠正策略的主要手段。但现有遥操作系统多仅依赖视觉闭环，且围绕平行夹爪设计，限制了机器人的可执行动作与操作者的表达能力。在共享自主场景下问题尤为突出：操作者只能通过被遮挡的相机观察场景，需在中途接管灵巧手，且往往已有物体被抓住。

Method: 提出 DITTO-X，一种手部无关的灵巧遥操作接口：渲染来自机器人手部已有传感器的关节级力与指尖接触事件，无需针对每种手重新设计即可驱动 Sharpa、Wuji、Inspire 三款商用灵巧手；由于外骨骼是主动驱动的，还支持反向遥操作，让机器人反驱动操作者手指进入自身构型，再移交控制权，使人在接管时已与所继承的状态匹配。

Result: 相比商用手部跟踪手套，DITTO-X 在常规数据采集以及在策略部署期间进行人类干预的接触丰富操作任务中，均提升了演示质量与吞吐量。

Conclusion: DITTO-X 通过力反馈与手部无关设计，改善了灵巧遥操作与共享自主中的人机交接体验，为接触丰富操作任务的数据采集与部署干预提供了更有效的方案。

Abstract: Teleoperated demonstrations are a primary source of data for robot manipulation, and teleoperated interventions are a primary mechanism for correcting policies at deployment. Yet most teleoperation systems close the loop through vision alone and are built around parallel-jaw grippers, limiting both what the robot can execute and what the operator can express through it. This is most damaging in shared autonomy, where the operator sees the scene only through occluded cameras and must take over a dexterous hand mid-task, often with an object already grasped. We present DITTO-X, a hand-agnostic dexterous teleoperation interface that renders joint-level force and fingertip contact events from sensing already on the robot hand, and drives three commercial dexterous hands (Sharpa, Wuji, and Inspire) without per-hand redesign. Because the exoskeleton is actuated, DITTO-X also supports reverse teleoperation, in which the robot back-drives the operator's fingers into its own configuration before control is transferred, so the human enters the loop already matched to the state they inherit. Our results show that DITTO-X improves demonstration quality and throughput over a commercial hand-tracking glove, both in regular data collection and in human intervention during policy deployment for contact-rich manipulation tasks. More information can be found from our website: https://tml.stanford.edu/ditto-x/.

</details>


### [197] [ECoMEM: Explicit Concept Memory for Memory-Dependent Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00801)
*Yize Liu,Ke Wang,Mac Schwager,Yiqing Xu,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出显式概念记忆 ECoMEM，将"维护基于证据的过去记录"与"学习如何行动"分离，用可复用概念库为 VLA 策略提供记忆接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 策略在长程机器人任务中一旦所需信息从当前观测中消失就会失败，记忆因此至关重要。现有方法或提供更长历史，或从观测-动作轨迹中隐式学习记忆，但动作监督只告诉策略如何行动，并未说明哪些过去事实应被保留、以及如何随新证据更新。

Method: 将记忆维护与动作学习解耦：用可复用的、有基础的概念库表示任务相关历史。基于证据的 Writer 负责选择并更新这些记忆记录，学习得到的 Reader 将其转化为记忆 token，直接作为条件输入 VLA。

Result: 在 16 个 RoboMME 任务中，ECoMEM 在 15 个任务上领先所评估的机器人策略。在两个新的真实机器人任务上，同一记忆库或可直接迁移、或仅需一个新概念，达到 86.1% 成功率，而无记忆 VLA 仅 8.6%。

Conclusion: 显式概念为机器人控制提供了一种可复用、可扩展的记忆接口。

Abstract: A robot may lose sight of an object it must later retrieve, need to recall what a person demonstrated earlier, or track which steps of a task it has already completed. Current vision-language-action (VLA) policies often fail once the information needed for action disappears from the current observation, making memory critical for long-horizon robot behavior. Existing approaches typically provide longer histories or learn implicit memory from observation-action trajectories. But action supervision tells a policy how to act, not what to remember: it does not specify which past facts should persist or how they should change as new evidence arrives. We therefore separate maintaining an evidence-grounded account of the past from learning how to act on it. This insight motivates Explicit Concept Memory (ECoMEM), which represents task-relevant history with a reusable library of grounded concepts. An evidence-based Writer selects and updates these records, while a learned Reader turns them into memory tokens that directly condition the VLA. Across 16 RoboMME tasks, ECoMEM leads the evaluated robot policies on 15 tasks. On two new real-robot tasks, the same memory library either transfers directly or requires only one new concept, achieving 86.1% success versus 8.6% for a no-memory VLA. These results show that explicit concepts provide a reusable and extensible memory interface for robot control. Project website: https://ecomem.github.io/

</details>


### [198] [Getting Out and Getting Back: World and Behavior Grounding in Real2Sim2Real Co-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00821)
*Samuel Liu,Youngsun Kim,Martin Matak,Gilwoo Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文区分"世界接地"与"行为接地",通过real2sim2real流程独立变化二者并联合训练,发现二者对策略性能有互补作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真可弥补真实演示数据的稀缺,用于联合训练,但世界保真度与对人类行为的相似度如何影响策略性能尚不清楚。

Method: 构建real2sim2real流程,独立调节世界接地(使仿真与真实系统对齐)与行为接地(使仿真轨迹与人类运动对齐)两条轴,生成数据用于联合训练;在动态灵巧抓取分拣任务上评估,并通过潜空间分析检验部署策略行为。

Result: 完全接地的联合训练将成功率从52%提升至86%;平均而言,世界接地提升18个百分点,行为接地提升10个百分点。部署策略表现为真实与仿真来源策略的混合:在已覆盖状态模仿真实演示,其余状态依赖仿真行为。

Conclusion: 世界接地使策略能利用超出真实数据覆盖范围的仿真经验,行为接地主要在世界接地不完美时起作用,二者角色互补;接地仿真在联合训练基础模型时仍有收益。

Abstract: Simulation can expand scarce real demonstrations for co-training, yet how world fidelity and similarity to human behavior affect policy performance remains unclear. We distinguish world grounding, which aligns simulation with the real system, and behavior grounding, which aligns simulated trajectories with human motion. We build a real2sim2real pipeline that varies these axes independently to generate data for co-training. On a dynamic dexterous pick-and-sort task, fully grounded co-training raises success from 52% to 86%; averaged across configurations, world grounding improves success by 18 percentage points and behavior grounding by 10. Deployed policies behave like a mixture of real-derived and simulation-derived policies, imitating real demonstrations in covered states and relying on simulated behavior elsewhere, which we examine through latent-space analysis. Together, these results suggest complementary roles: world grounding lets policies use simulated experience beyond real-data coverage, while behavior grounding matters mainly when world grounding is imperfect. Grounded simulation remains beneficial when co-training foundation models.

</details>


### [199] [TRACE: Privacy-Preserving Next-Best-View Selection over Distributed 3D Gaussian-Splat Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00822)
*Amirhossein Mollaei Khass,Athanasios Cosse,Qiyu Sun,Nader Motee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TRACE协议：多机器人各自维护私有3DGS地图，通过交换仅两个射线量（透射率与辐亮度深度分箱聚合）实现去中心化的下一最佳视角选择，无需共享地图，消息量与地图规模无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多机器人建图场景下，每个机器人需基于"全局信息"评估候选视角的信息增益，但地图是私有且不可共享的。期望信息增益取决于其他机器人地图带来的遮挡与背景辐射，必须相对于池化地图评估，而没有任何单个机器人拥有该地图。因此需要一种既能保持地图私密、又能精确重建集中式增益的通信方案。

Method: 证明耦合仅通过两个射线量传递：某splat前方的透射率与后方的辐亮度，二者均为该射线上命中点的求和。因此按机器人可分解：每个机器人沿候选视角的射线、在自己地图的深度分箱上求和，并将求和值及位姿导数发送出去；规划视角的机器人据此计算其EIG及SO(3)上的梯度。协议命名为TRACE（Transmittance and Radiance Aggregates for the EIG）。

Result: 证明该重建在splat后方的深度分箱不混合两个机器人的命中时是精确的，并给出其他情形下的误差上界。在Habitat-Sim上进行100多次下一最佳视角决策，TRACE在83.3%的情况下选出的朝向与集中式方案相差15度以内，其视角达到集中式EIG的97.9%。

Conclusion: TRACE实现了隐私保护下的去中心化下一最佳视角选择，不共享splat且消息大小不随地图增长，精度接近集中式基准，验证了射线量的可分解性带来的通信效率与准确性。

Abstract: Share the light, not the map. We study next-best-view selection for a team of robots, each of which builds its own 3D Gaussian Splatting map and keeps it private. A robot picks the view with the largest expected information gain (EIG) about the splats along its own path. This gain depends on the other maps. Their splats occlude its own and shine behind them, so the gain has to be evaluated against the pooled map. No robot has this map. We show that the coupling passes through only two ray quantities, the transmittance in front of a splat and the radiance behind it, and that both are sums over the hits of the ray. Hence, they decompose across the robots, and each robot sums them over depth bins in its own map, along the rays of a candidate view, and sends the sums with their pose derivatives. The robot planning the view turns them into its EIG and gradient on SO(3). Transmittance and Radiance Aggregates, communicated for the EIG, give the protocol its name: TRACE. No robot shares its splats, and the message size does not grow with a map. We prove that the reconstruction is exact unless a depth bin behind a splat mixes hits of two robots, and we bound the error otherwise. Over 100 next-best-view decisions in Habitat-Sim, TRACE picks a heading within 15 degrees of the centralized one in 83.3% of the cases, and its views reach 97.9% of the centralized EIG.

</details>


### [200] [Reactive Humanoid Multi-Contact Using Learned Stability Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00823)
*Stephen McCrory,Beomyeong Park,Nicholas Kitchel,Nehar Poddar,Robert Griffin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种利用手部接触的实时规划与控制方法，在低稳定场景下稳定人形机器人，仿真与实机实验均表明可显著提升抗扰动能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅依靠足部接触在低稳定场景下可能导致人形机器人跌倒，本文希望借助手部接触进行反应式稳定，从而提升机器人的抗扰动与恢复能力。

Method: 在机器人可达工作空间内采样候选接触点；通过质心动量动力学在撞击前、撞击、撞击后三阶段展开进行预演；依据撞击后阶段的压力中心（CoP）控制能力对采样点打分；核心是学习到的撞击后 CoP 区域模型，以快速评估候选接触点；规划分两阶段：先选最优支撑区域，再在区域内计算最优支撑点。

Result: 仿真结果显示，相比不使用手部接触的恢复，冲量韧性平均提升 89%，相比朴素规划策略（最近可达区域）提升 17%；实机站立推动测试中，相比朴素手部放置，稳定时间平均减少 43%，行走测试相比无手部接触的基线恢复减少 18%。

Conclusion: 学习到的撞击后 CoP 模型结合两阶段规划，可快速且有效地选择手部支撑点，为仅靠足部难以稳定的场景提供实用的人形机器人稳定方案，并在仿真与硬件上得到验证。

Abstract: We present a planning and control approach to reactively use hand contacts to stabilize a humanoid in low stability scenarios, where only using feet contacts may result in a fall. Candidate contacts are sampled within the robot's reachable workspace, and a preview is computed by rolling out the centroidal dynamics through pre-impact, impact and post-impact phases. Sampled points are scored based on the Center of Pressure (CoP) control authority at the post-impact phase. Central to our approach is a learned model of the robot's CoP region during post-impact, which enables rapid evaluation of candidate contact points compared to traditional optimization-based methods. The presented planner has two stages: the first selects an optimal bracing region and the second computes an optimal bracing point within the region. Our simulation results demonstrate an average increase in impulse resilience of 89% over recovery without hand contacts and 17% over a naive planning strategy (closest reachable region). We validate our framework on hardware, performing push tests while standing and walking. The standing trials show an average 43% reduction in stabilization time compared to naive hand placement and the walking trials demonstrate a 18% reduction compared to baseline recovery (without hand contacts).

</details>


### [201] [Are Frontier VLM Agents Ready to Be Robot Generalists? An Empirical Study with the Embodied Agent Arena](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00854)
*Haojian Huang,Pukun Zhao,Zexi Li,Yehang Zhang,Yangkai Wei,Wenqian Li,Han Yang,Kaiwen Zhou,Ying-Cong Chen,Yinchuan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出"Embodied Agent Arena"，一个涵盖几何、空间推理、可供性、任务规划与操作的1000案例评测平台，用于检验视觉语言模型在完整机器人任务中的局部能力与整体任务成功之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿视觉语言模型集成了场景估计、交互接地与可执行动作能力，但尚不清楚这些局部能力是否能支撑完整的机器人任务，因此需要系统评估其作为"机器人通才"的成熟度。

Method: 构建包含1000个案例的评测竞技场，来源涵盖32个既有数据集及新基准GeoProbe（基于Blender渲染与真实场景图像的几何估计）。采用最小化测试框架，保留原始观测与操作，同时分离度量精度、功能接地与原生目标完成度。评估7个VLM，分析Astra的任务特定优势，并比较更丰富观测的执行协议与多轮审查。

Result: Astra在精确估计与可用接触定位方面优势最强，但在完成协调性、目标导向的动作方面仍存在明显不足。

Conclusion: 局部能力的优势并不能直接转化为完整任务成功，实现协调一致的目标导向操作仍是机器人通才化的关键缺口。

Abstract: Frontier vision-language models (VLMs) combine scene estimation, interaction grounding, and executable actions. Understanding how these abilities support complete robotic tasks is central to evaluating their readiness as robot generalists. We introduce Embodied Agent Arena to examine where local competence supports, or falls short of, complete task success across Geometry, Spatial Reasoning, Affordance, Task Planning, and Manipulation. The arena contains 1,000 cases drawn from 32 established sources and GeoProbe, our new benchmark for geometric estimation on Blender renders and real-scene images. A minimal harness preserves source observations and operations while separating metric precision, functional grounding, and native goal completion. We evaluate seven VLMs, analyze Astra's task-specific advantages, and compare richer-observation execution protocols and multi-round review. Across the arena, Astra's advantage is strongest in precise estimation and usable-contact localization; completing coordinated, goal-directed actions remains the key gap to robot generalism.

</details>


### [202] [Kinematic MeanFlow: One-Step Action Generation Policy for Robotic Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00864)
*Jiawei Fan,Sifeng Wang,Yuqing Hou,Anbang Yao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Kinematic MeanFlow (K-MF)，一种面向机器人基础模型(RFM)的一步动作生成策略，通过运动学恒等式解耦MeanFlow中的时间导数项，解决了直接应用MeanFlow导致的性能崩溃问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多步flow matching在RFM中推理延迟高，而直接用MeanFlow实现一步生成会导致性能崩溃。研究发现根源在于RFM速度场的两个特性：局部加速度在去噪早期稳定但末期急剧上升，以及样本间幅值离散度随去噪进程扩大。

Method: 提出K-MF，基于运动学恒等式将MeanFlow公式中的时间导数项解耦为被中间点分隔的两个子区间项，分别捕捉早期和晚期去噪动态，同时缓解过程中的误差放大。支持从头训练和微调两种范式。

Result: K-MF在多种任务上实现一步动作生成，多数场景下优于多步flow matching。推理效率上，将GR00T-N1.6的动作头延迟在L40和Jetson Orin上（eager与compiled模式）降低67.5%~74.4%，端到端延迟降低30.3%~54.9%。

Conclusion: K-MF有效赋能RFM实现高效一步动作生成，在大幅降低推理延迟的同时保持甚至超越多步方法的性能。

Abstract: In this paper, we study how to achieve one-step action generation in Robotic Foundation Models (RFMs), aiming to overcome the high inference latency of multi-step flow matching. MeanFlow provides a promising framework for this goal, yet its direct application leads to performance collapse. We discover that this stems from two distinctive dynamics exhibited in the RFM velocity field: (1) the ``local acceleration" exhibits stability early on, but surges sharply towards the end of the denoising process, and (2) the spread of its magnitudes across samples widens as denoising progresses. To address these issues, we introduce Kinematic MeanFlow (K-MF), a novel one-step action policy tailored for RFMs. Specifically, grounded in a kinematic identity, K-MF decouples the time derivative term in the MeanFlow formulation into two sub-interval terms separated by an intermediate point. This decoupled formulation enables the two terms to capture early-stage and late-stage denoising dynamics, respectively, while mitigating the error amplification across the process. As a result, our K-MF empowers RFMs to achieve one-step action generation in both training from scratch and fine-tuning paradigms across diverse tasks, while outperforming multi-step flow matching in most settings. In terms of inference efficiency, K-MF reduces action-head latency of GR00T-N1.6 by 67.5%~74.4% across L40 and Jetson Orin in eager and compiled modes, yielding end-to-end latency reductions of 30.3%~54.9%. Code will be available at https://github.com/IntelChina-AI/K-MF.

</details>


### [203] [UniTrackPLA: Unified Panorama-Language-Action Model for Instruction-Guided Navigation and Dynamic Person Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00878)
*Pengfei Qi,Haoran Lin,Sizhuang Chen,Kai Luo,Sirui Zhang,Xinqi Liu,Fei Cheng,Wenrui Chen,Liming Yin,Kailun Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出统一的全景-语言-动作模型 UniTrackPLA，用于指令导航与动态人员跟踪，并构建了 OmniTrackNav-Bench 基准，在仿真与真实机器人上验证了全景闭环控制的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常依赖前向观测，并将导航与人员跟踪用独立策略处理，限制了全景感知能力与统一的闭环控制；同时需要在任意初始目标方位下同时支持语言指定目标导航与动态人员跟踪。

Method: 提出 UniTrackPLA：包含全景感知编码 PAE，保留全景投影透视图的时序与方位结构，使透视预训练视觉编码器可处理全景输入；共享视觉-语言骨干将指令落到全景上下文并预测连续机器人中心路点块；世界-动作一致性 WAC 预测动作条件下的未来视觉状态并在线上验证路点前缀，可复用可靠动作、不一致时触发重规划；同时构建 OmniTrackNav-Bench 基准。

Result: 跟踪整体 SR 从 23.50% 提升至 35.00%；Omni-VLN SR/SPL 从 13.00%/12.77% 提升至 19.75%/19.29%；加入 76 条真实路线后，留出集 EP@0.2m 从 42.92% 提升至 92.08%；在 Go2-W 机器人上完成室内外统一全景跟踪与导航闭环实验。基准包含 5,000 条仿真跟踪轨迹、10,000 条仿真 VLN 路线与 96 条验证过的真实路线，提供 919,978 个路点监督样本。

Conclusion: UniTrackPLA 通过全景感知编码与世界-动作一致性机制，实现了导航与人员跟踪的统一闭环控制，并在仿真与真实机器人实验中验证了有效性与可迁移性。

Abstract: General-purpose embodied robots should support both navigation toward language-specified destinations and dynamic person tracking under arbitrary initial target azimuths. However, existing methods typically rely on forward-facing observations and address these tasks with separate policies, limiting omnidirectional perception and unified closed-loop control. We present UniTrackPLA, a unified panorama-language-action model for instruction-guided navigation and dynamic person tracking. Its Panoramic-Aware Encoding (PAE) preserves the temporal and azimuthal structure of perspective views projected from each panorama, enabling perspective-pretrained visual encoders to process omnidirectional observations. A shared vision-language backbone grounds instructions in the panoramic context and predicts continuous robot-centric waypoint chunks for both tasks. World-Action Consistency (WAC) further predicts action-conditioned future visual states and verifies waypoint prefixes online, allowing reliable actions to be reused while triggering replanning upon inconsistency. We also introduce OmniTrackNav-Bench, comprising 5,000 simulated tracking trajectories, 10,000 simulated VLN routes, and 96 verified real-world routes, providing 919,978 waypoint-supervision instances. UniTrackPLA improves overall tracking SR from 23.50% to 35.00% and Omni-VLN SR/SPL from 13.00%/12.77% to 19.75%/19.29%. Incorporating 76 real-world routes further improves held-out EP@0.2m from 42.92% to 92.08%. Closed-loop experiments on a Go2-W robot demonstrate unified panoramic tracking and navigation across indoor and outdoor environments. The project page is at https://tw5775.github.io/UniTrackPLA.

</details>


### [204] [Real-Time Human-Adaptive Task Allocation for Multi-Human Multi-Robot Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00897)
*Seabin Lee,Sujeong Park,Nayoung Kim,Sungjin Park,Haechan Jung,Changjoo Nam*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种考虑人为因素的多机器人监督任务分配方法，动态调节操作员监督能力以平衡工作负荷、防止过载并提升团队表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多操作员偶尔远程操作多机器人时，任务分配需考虑个体操作员实时的认知状态，但现有方法多假设每位操作员的监督能力固定，忽略工作负荷与疲劳等人为因素波动，导致负荷分布不均——部分操作员过载，另一些则未被充分利用。

Method: 方法动态调节监督能力并按任务优先级分配任务，采用贪心策略在任务优先级下最小化估计的操作员工作负荷；机器人依据操作员当前监督能力进行指派，所需投入取决于任务类型。

Result: 用户研究中，按预定义时间间隔分析表明，与不考虑人为因素的基线方法相比，所提方法持续获得更高绩效和更低疲劳行为迹象。

Conclusion: 结果表明自适应能力调节是维持操作员在长时间、高需求场景下表现的有效预防机制。

Abstract: We propose a human-factor-aware method of allocating robot supervision tasks to multiple human operators. In scenarios where multiple operators occasionally teleoperate multiple robots to help the robots overcome difficulties, the allocation of the supervisory control tasks to humans needs to consider the real-time cognitive states of individual operators. However, most existing methods assume fixed supervisory capacity per operator and overlook fluctuations in the human factors such as workload and fatigue. As a result, workload distribution can be unbalanced where some operators become overloaded while the others remain underused. Our method dynamically regulates supervisory capacity and allocates tasks in a way that maintains balanced mental workload, prevents overload, and improves overall team performance. The allocation method uses a greedy strategy that minimizes estimated operator workloads with task prioritization. Robots are assigned to operators by reflecting their current supervisory capacity where the required effort depends on the types of tasks. In the user study, the analysis across predefined time intervals shows that the proposed method consistently achieves higher performance and lower behavioral signs of fatigue compared to a baseline method that does not consider human factors. These results highlight adaptive capacity adjustment as an effective preventive mechanism for sustaining operator performance in long-duration, high-demand settings.

</details>


### [205] [TOAST: Stochastic Robot Action Tokenization for Autoregressive Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00899)
*Keisuke Shirai,Tomohiro Motoda,Hanbit Oh,Ryoichi Nakajo,Roman Mykhailyshyn,Ryo Hanai,Shotaro Miwa,Yukiyasu Domae*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TOAST，一种随机动作分词方法，在策略训练时对同一量化动作序列采样不同的分词形式，以利用表示冗余提升自回归VLA策略学习效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FAST虽能紧凑编码动作序列减少token数量，但并未提升有限演示下的策略学习效率。原因是FAST对每个量化动作序列只给出单一确定的分词，而同一机器人运动其实可由多个token序列表示并解码得到，这种表示冗余未被利用。

Method: TOAST在策略训练过程中，对同一量化动作序列随机采样不同的分词方案，从而在不改变底层机器人动作、不增加演示数据的前提下，丰富离散监督信号。

Result: 在LIBERO上，TOAST一致优于确定性方法，且训练数据越少提升越明显，仅用1/16训练数据时成功率提升6.8个百分点；在四项真实机器人操作任务上，平均成功率较确定性方法提升15.8个百分点。

Conclusion: 随机动作分词能有效提升自回归机器人策略学习，尤其在训练数据有限时效果显著。

Abstract: Autoregressive Vision-Language-Action models often represent continuous robot actions as discrete token sequences, enabling action prediction with standard next-token objectives. FAST has substantially improved this representation by compactly encoding action containing diverse temporal frequencies into relatively few tokens. However, while such compression reduces the number of action tokens required for autoregressive prediction, it does not necessarily improve the efficiency of policy learning from limited demonstrations. In particular, FAST typically assigns a single deterministic tokenization to each quantized action sequence, although multiple token sequences can represent and decode to the same robot motion. We investigate whether exploiting this representational redundancy can improve policy learning. In this paper, we propose TOkenization of Action sequences with STochastic sampling (TOAST), a stochastic action tokenization method that samples alternative tokenizations of the same quantized action sequence during policy training. This diversifies the discrete supervision while preserving the underlying robot action and requires no additional demonstrations. Experiments on LIBERO show that TOAST consistently improves over its deterministic counterpart, with the improvement increasing as training data decreases, achieving a 6.8 point gain in success rate when only 1/16 of training data is available. Across four real-robot manipulation tasks, TOAST further improves mean success rate by 15.8 points over the deterministic counterpart. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of stochastic action tokenization for autoregressive robot policy learning, particularly when training data are limited.

</details>


### [206] [Screw Attention: Rigid-Body Algebra Inside a Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00904)
*Aly Magassouba*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 Screw Attention，一种将两刚体间关系建模为空间变换而非图边的 transformer 层，仅需 16,162 参数即在 LIBERO-Spatial 达到 97.3% 成功率，且对坐标系约定变化完全鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习到的操作策略需要从数据中重新发现刚体力学本可闭式给出的空间关系，这既耗费数据，又使策略对场景几何变化脆弱。作者希望将几何结构直接嵌入网络架构，以减少数据依赖并提升对坐标系与几何变化的鲁棒性。

Method: 每个 token 表示一个刚体并携带位姿；每对 token 携带相对位姿，对机器人关节则携带关节螺旋（screw）。消息沿该空间关系传递到接收者坐标系，而注意力分数只使用坐标系不变的量。该设计保证消息对每个 token 独立的坐标系变换具有等变性，且单层即可表达刚体力学的速度递推。

Result: 在模拟操作任务上，Screw Attention 匹配或超越同规模对照（图网络、transformer、扁平网络），在 LIBERO-Spatial 上以 16,162 参数从物体位姿（无图像与语言）达到 97.3%，优于参数量多 27 倍的扁平网络。改变每连杆坐标系约定时其成功率不变，而其他学习网络均降至 3% 以下。作为门控残差叠加在解析控制器上，使插入成功率提升 17.3 个百分点；对 10mm 以内的位姿噪声及 Franka 机械臂出厂标定范围内的关节偏移均不敏感。

Conclusion: 结果表明存在一条判据：当任务需要系统其他部分无法提供的坐标系间关系时，几何结构是决定性的。作者将发布代码与训练好的策略。

Abstract: Learned manipulation policies rediscover from data the spatial relations that rigid-body mechanics supplies in closed form. This costs data, and it leaves the policies fragile to geometric changes in the scene. We present Screw Attention, a transformer layer in which the relation between two bodies is a spatial transform rather than a graph edge. Every token is a body with a pose. Each pair of tokens carries the relative pose and, for robot joints, the joint screw. Messages are transported along this relation into the receiver's frame, while the attention scores see only frame-invariant quantities. By construction, the messages are equivariant to an independent change of frame at every token, and a single layer can express the velocity recursion of rigid-body mechanics. On simulated manipulation tasks, Screw Attention matches or outperforms controls of the same size, including graph, transformer and flat networks on LIBERO-Spatial. With 16,162 parameters it reaches 97.3% on LIBERO-Spatial from object poses (without images or language), above a flat network with 27x more parameters. Under a change of per-link frame convention its success is unchanged, while every other learned network falls below 3%. Placed on an analytic controller as a gated residual, it raises insertion success by 17.3 points. It is unaffected by pose noise up to 10,mm and by joint offsets within the factory calibration of a Franka arm. These results suggest a criterion: geometry is decisive when the task requires relations between frames that no other part of the system supplies. Code and trained policies will be released.

</details>


### [207] [eRLT: Efficient VLA Reinforcement Learning via Action-Relevant Token Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00913)
*Dehao Huang,Jianbang Liu,Jianpan Gao,Chao Tang,Zilang Cen,Zedong Dan,Jiaheng Wang,Tingguang Li,Yue Wang,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: eRLT 通过在被冻结的 VLA 模型中跨 token 与层级路由任务相关的动作信息，构建高效状态表示以提升在线强化学习的样本效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 VLA 模型高效适配到下游任务具有挑战。现有在线 RL 方法依赖冻结 VLA，其样本效率取决于 actor 和 critic 所用状态表示质量。而现有表示要么用与 VLA 无关的视觉编码器，要么对 VLA 内部表示做固定压缩，均未显式提取对下游动作精修与动作价值估计最有用、任务特定的动作相关 VLA 特征。

Method: 提出 eRLT：在冻结 VLA 的 token 与层之间路由任务特定的动作相关信息。学习到的路由 token 动态聚合多个深度的视觉-语言特征，轻量级层路由器将这些摘要组合成固定维度的 RL token。路由模块先用专家演示初始化以捕捉可预测专家动作的特征，再用在线交互中的 critic 反馈进行精修，用于动作价值估计。

Result: 在 7 个 LIBERO 与 RoboTwin 任务上，平均归一化学习曲线 AUC 相比代表性基线最高提升 23.7%。在 USB 连接器插入与主板排线插入的真实机器人实验中，AUC 分别比最强基线提升 108.9% 和 46.7%。

Conclusion: eRLT 通过跨 token 与层级的自适应路由显式提取任务相关的动作特征，结合专家演示初始化与 critic 反馈精修，显著提升冻结 VLA 在线 RL 适配的样本效率，并在仿真与真实机器人任务上验证了有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models provide strong behavioral priors for robotic manipulation, yet efficiently adapting them to downstream tasks remains challenging. Recent work addresses this challenge by adapting frozen VLAs through online reinforcement learning (RL), whose sample efficiency depends on the quality of the state representation used by the actor and critic. Existing methods construct such representations either with VLA-independent visual encoders or through fixed compression of internal VLA representations. Neither design explicitly extracts the task-specific action-relevant VLA features most useful for downstream action refinement and action-value estimation, therefore limiting sample efficiency. To address this limitation, we introduce eRLT, which constructs an effective state representation by routing task-specific action-relevant information across both tokens and layers of the frozen VLA. Specifically, learned routing tokens dynamically aggregate visual-language features at multiple depths, while a lightweight layer router combines these summaries into a fixed-dimensional RL token. The routing module is initialized using expert demonstrations to capture features predictive of expert actions and then refined using critic feedback from online interactions for action-value estimation. Across seven LIBERO and RoboTwin tasks, eRLT improves mean normalized learning-curve AUC by up to 23.7% over representative baselines. Real-robot experiments on USB connector insertion and motherboard ribbon-cable insertion further show AUC improvements of 108.9% and 46.7%, respectively, over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [208] [A Survey on End-to-End Autonomous Driving Training from the Perspectives of Data, Strategy, and Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00926)
*Chengkai Xu,Yiming Cui,Jiaqi Liu,Yicheng Guo,Cheng Qin,Geyuan Zhang,Xinwei Dong,Shiyu Fang,Peng Hang,Jian Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对端到端自动驾驶（E2E-AD）的训练方法与生态系统进行了综述，提出“数据-策略-平台”三层分类体系，并展望未来发展方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端学习已成为自动驾驶的变革性范式，但其有效性最终取决于训练生态系统的质量，因此有必要系统梳理E2E-AD的训练方法与生态。

Method: 提出“数据-策略-平台”分类法，将训练视为相互依赖的系统：数据层定义可学习内容，策略层决定学习如何对齐驾驶目标，平台层支撑可扩展性与持续演进；并据此综述数据处理流程、学习范式与训练基础设施的最新进展及其相互作用。

Result: 系统分析了各层方法如何共同影响模型的性能、鲁棒性与可部署性，并总结了当前研究的局限性。

Conclusion: 提出前瞻性愿景：从数据量转向数据价值、从孤立优化转向基础模型驱动的泛化、从静态训练转向训练-测试一体化闭环，以实现鲁棒、可扩展且可信的自动驾驶系统。

Abstract: Autonomous driving is a cornerstone technology for the future of intelligent transportation, where end-to-end learning has emerged as a transformative paradigm that directly maps multimodal sensory inputs to driving actions through unified differentiable models. While offering advantages, the effectiveness of end-to-end autonomous driving (E2E-AD) is ultimately determined by the quality of its training ecosystem. This paper provides a comprehensive review of training methods and ecosystem for E2E-AD. We introduce a Data-Strategy-Platform taxonomy that conceptualizes training as an interdependent system. The data layer defines what can be learned, the strategy layer governs how learning aligns with driving objectives, and the platform layer supports scalability and continuous evolution. Within this framework, we survey recent advances across data-centric pipelines, learning paradigms, and training infrastructures, and analyze their interplay in shaping model performance, robustness, and deployability. Finally, we reflect on current limitations and articulate a forward-looking vision that emphasizes a shift from data quantity to data value, from isolated optimization to foundation-driven generalization, and from static training to integrated training-testing loops, aiming toward robust, scalable, and trustworthy autonomous driving systems. We maintain a continuously updated repository tracking cutting-edge literature and works at \href{https://github.com/Jiaaqiliu/Awesome-Training-Ecosystem-for-E2E-AD}{Our Project Page}.

</details>


### [209] [ShowerFlex: Achieving Pseudo-Static Balancing in a Continuum Shower Hose](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00936)
*Zhiyu Ren,Girish Krishnan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种用于辅助老年人洗浴的高自由度准静态平衡连续体机构（模块化球窝关节+弹簧卷线器），实现无电力的"推即停"交互，兼具重力补偿与低操作力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球老龄化背景下，老年人维持洗浴等日常生活活动独立性成为难题。现有坐姿洗浴设备仅有刚性顶置花洒或需持续握持的手持软管，操作不便且易致跌倒风险，缺乏安全、低成本、易用的辅助方案。

Method: 提出模块化连续体架构，由摩擦锁定的球窝关节（loc-line）与可伸缩弹簧卷线器组成，实现重力补偿；建立递归正运动学框架构建几何模型，并进行涵盖51自由度的静态平衡分析；采用蒙特卡洛数值仿真验证形态适应性与稳定性；最后通过物理样机实验检验实际性能。

Result: 仿真验证了系统在标准洗浴空间内多种极端位形下的形态适应性与稳定性；物理实验证实样机对重力具有内在静态稳定性，操作力处于老年人人体工效能力范围之内。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种低成本、本质安全的淋浴喷头设计，可减少体力负担，恢复老年人在个人卫生方面的尊严与自主性。

Abstract: With the global population rapidly aging, maintaining independence in Activities of Daily Living (ADLs), particularly bathing or showering, has become a critical challenge. Older adults who sit while showering currently have limited options, often relying on rigid overhead showerheads or flexible hand-held hoses that require continuous gripping and precarious user maneuvers, increasing the risk of falls. To address this need, this paper introduces a highly articulated, pseudo-static balanced continuum mechanism designed specifically for accessible bathing assistance. The proposed mechanism features a modular continuum architecture composed of friction-locked ball-and-socket joints (loc-line), seamlessly integrated with a retractable spring-loaded reel for effective gravity compensation. This hybrid design achieves an intuitive "Push-and-Stay" interaction logic, maintaining an approximate static equilibrium with ergonomically low actuation force, without any external electronic power, while significantly minimizing the force needed for repositioning. Theoretically, we established a recursive forward kinematics framework to construct the geometric model of the structure, coupled with a comprehensive static equilibrium analysis to evaluate the system's holding capacity across its 51-DOF structure. Numerical simulations utilizing Monte Carlo methods validate the system's morphological adaptability and stability at various extreme positions within a standard bath space. Physical experiments further validate the prototype's real-world performance, confirming its intrinsic static stability against gravity and ensuring that the actuation force remains well within the ergonomic capabilities of older adults. Ultimately, this research provides a low-cost, intrinsically safe showerhead design that reduces physical strain, restoring dignity and autonomy in personal hygiene.

</details>


### [210] [NarrativeFlow: Flow-Based Vision-Language-Action Model Using Robot Velocity Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00981)
*Shota Kobayashi,Koki Seno,Daichi Yashima,Komei Sugiura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 NarrativeFlow，将机器人流建模为语言条件下的连续速度场，用流匹配生成物理一致的机器人流，实现跨本体、运动中心的语言条件操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言条件操作对实际机器人系统至关重要，但扩展机器人基础模型受限于本体特定数据的人工采集成本。机器人流可作跨本体的运动中心表示以利用多平台数据，然而现有方法要么用稀疏关键点位移粗略近似机器人流，要么无法处理语言条件操作。

Method: NarrativeFlow 以流匹配（flow-matching）框架，在语言条件下将机器人流建模为连续速度场，从而生成与真实操作物理一致的机器人流。

Result: 在语言条件操作标准数据集上，NarrativeFlow 在标准评测指标上优于代表性基线方法；真实世界实验中，在多个操作任务上成功率高于基线方法。

Conclusion: NarrativeFlow 通过连续速度场与流匹配实现语言条件机器人流生成，验证了其在标准基准与真实场景中的有效性，为跨本体数据利用提供可行路径。

Abstract: We focus on language-conditioned flow-based manipulation, where robot flows (robot velocity fields) serve as embodiment-agnostic, motion-centric representations for leveraging data collected from multiple robot platforms.
