This task is crucial because language-conditioned manipulation is essential for practical robotic systems, yet scaling robot foundation models remains limited by the labor-intensive collection of embodiment-specific data. Existing methods either coarsely approximate robot flows with sparse keypoint displacements, or cannot handle language-conditioned manipulation. To address this limitation, we propose NarrativeFlow, which models robot flows as continuous velocity fields using a flow-matching formulation conditioned on language. Accordingly, NarrativeFlow generates robot flows that are physically consistent with real-world manipulation. To validate NarrativeFlow, we have conducted experiments on standard datasets for language-conditioned manipulation. The experimental results show that NarrativeFlow outperforms representative baseline methods on standard evaluation metrics. Furthermore, through real-world experiments, we show that NarrativeFlow achieves higher success rates than baseline methods across multiple manipulation tasks. The project page is available at https://shota0520.github.io/NarrativeFlow-project-page/

</details>


### [211] [Divide-and-Remember: Recursive Action-Relevant Memory for Long-Horizon VLA Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.00982)
*Xuehui Yu,Eason Yu,Meiyi Wang,Haozhe Du,Stefano V. Albrecht,Harold Soh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文将VLA模型中的记忆内容选择建模为优化问题，证明最优记忆应最大化条件互信息I(a_t; m_t | o_t)，并提出递归记忆方法Divide-and-Remember (D&R)，在长时程操作任务上以仅64个token的预算取得最优表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在依赖历史信息的操作任务上表现不佳，因为仅凭当前观测无法确定动作，策略需要历史记忆。现有记忆方法大多通过人工设计来决定记忆内容（如保留像素变化大的帧），在不同任务上收益不一致。

Method: 作者从模仿学习的POMDP形式化出发，将"记住什么"视为优化问题，证明最优记忆应最大化动作与记忆在给定当前观测下的条件互信息I(a_t; m_t | o_t)，即保留当前观测未包含的、与动作相关的历史信息。据此提出Divide-and-Remember (D&R)递归记忆方法，学习记忆函数m_t = M(h_t)，包含两个策略：(1) 将全历史选择递归分解为在2K个token上做top-K选择的子问题，使端到端学习的固定大小轻量选择器可支持无限长历史；(2) 所有递归块共享同一选择器，以捕捉各块共有的选择规则并保持高效。

Result: 在RoboMME（包含16个长时程操作任务、需记忆何时/何地/做什么/如何行动的基准）上，D&R在仅64个token预算下取得最优平均成功率，并在全部四个套件上均有稳定提升；真实机器人实验也显示相同收益。

Conclusion: D&R通过将记忆选择形式化为条件互信息最大化，并采用递归、共享选择器的轻量设计，有效解决了VLA在历史依赖任务中的记忆问题，兼顾长上下文扩展性与计算效率。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models struggle on history-dependent manipulation tasks, where the current observation alone does not determine the action, and the policy needs a memory of the history. Existing memory methods decide what to remember by design, for example, keeping frames with large pixel changes, and show inconsistent gains across tasks. We view what to remember as an optimisation problem. From the POMDP formulation of imitation learning, we show that the optimal memory maximises the conditional mutual information $I(a_t; m_t \mid o_t)$ between the action and the memory given the current observation. Intuitively, this means preserving the action-relevant information in the history that is not already contained in the current observation. Based on our analysis, we propose Divide-and-Remember (D&R), a recursive memory method that learns a memory function $m_t = M(h_t)$ and scales to long contexts while staying compute-light. It involves two strategies: (1) the selection over the full history is divided recursively into subproblems of top-$K$ selection over $2K$ tokens, so that fixed-size, lightweight selectors learned end-to-end support an unbounded history; (2) all recursion blocks share one selector, which captures the selection rule common to every block and keeps the method efficient. On RoboMME, a benchmark of 16 long-horizon manipulation tasks that require remembering when, where, what, and how to act, D&R achieves a state-of-the-art average success rate with consistent gains across all four suites under a budget of only 64 tokens; real-robot experiments show the same gain. Code, checkpoints and more results are at https://dnr-memory.github.io/

</details>


### [212] [Online Planning for Sparse Ground Target Search from a High-Altitude UAV under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01067)
*Ashik E Rasul,Hyung-Jin Yoon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文针对高空无人机目标视场内但尺度过小难以观测的问题，提出将PTZ相机的顺序探索建模为POMDP并用POMCP求解，结合基于目标尺度的感知可靠性与选择性集成检测，在仿真中显著减少搜索步数并降低对传感器分辨率的依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高空无人机搜索地面目标时，目标虽在视场内却因表观尺度极小而难以观测；分辨率下采样与上下文受限使标准检测器表现不佳。主动目标搜索虽可引导无人机改变位姿获取更丰富信息，但城市空域飞行法规常限制其物理移动，因此需要一种不依赖大幅机动的替代主动感知方案。

Method: 将利用机载相机PTZ机制的顺序探索形式化为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP），智能体维护关于目标真实位置的信念状态；采用部分可观测蒙特卡洛规划（POMCP）求解，并将感知可靠性以目标物体尺度为条件进行建模，在候选位置确定后加入选择性集成检测作为额外推理步骤。

Result: 在照片级真实感仿真器中，于不同环境条件和飞行器状态下进行实验，能够检测不同尺度的地面目标；与基线方法相比，所用步数显著更少，且对传感器分辨率的依赖极小。

Conclusion: 基于PTZ的主动感知配合POMDP/POMCP规划与选择性集成检测，为受飞行法规约束下的无人机小尺度地面目标搜索提供了一种高效、低分辨率依赖的可行方案。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) searching for ground targets from a high altitude face a unique challenge, particularly when the target is already within the field of view but effectively unobservable because of its small apparent scale. Standard object detectors often underperform in such scenarios because of resolution downscaling and limited context. In contrast, active object search frameworks address this challenge by directing the agent to a suitable pose to gather richer visual information. However, flight regulations in urban airspace often restrict such physical movements for UAVs. As an alternative active sensing approach, the UAV can leverage the pan-tilt-zoom (PTZ) mechanism of the onboard camera to dynamically adjust its field of view and sequentially gather enhanced visual information from specific regions of interest. Once the candidate locations of the target are identified, it can deploy more expensive object detection schemes, such as an ensemble of multiple models, to get better reasoning at a fixed scale. In this work, we formulate the sequential exploration with PTZ operation as a partially observable Markov decision process (POMDP), in which the agent maintains a belief state over the target's true location. To solve the POMDP, we deploy partially observable Monte Carlo planning (POMCP), where we condition the sensing reliability on target object scale and deploy selective ensemble detection as an additional reasoning step.
  We validate our methodology with experiments in a photorealistic simulator under different environmental conditions and vehicle states, showing detection of ground targets at variable scales with significantly fewer steps and minimal dependence on sensor resolution compared to baseline methods.

</details>


### [213] [quARtet Marker: A 3D-Printable Multi-Tag Fiducial for Robust Near-Frontal Pose Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01072)
*Araki Wakiuchi,Hikaru Sasaki,Takamitsu Matsubara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出quARtet标记：在同一紧凑足迹内倾斜多个AprilTag，使各标签在正对相机时仍处于非正面视角，从而提升位姿估计精度并兼顾可抓取性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 透明或反光实验器皿的识别与定位困难。编码平面基准标记是实用的改造方案，易打印且保持标记面平整可抓。但单个平面标签在近正面视角下透视线索消失，最不可靠；非平面几何虽能恢复透视线索，却会侵占平行夹爪需要接触的平面。

Method: 提出quARtet三维可打印基准标记：四个倾斜AprilTag的全部检测角点进入同一次PnP求解，共享的配置同时定义制造几何与检测器模型。因倾斜占用平面面积，设计三种布局，在位姿估计一致性与可抓取性之间权衡。开展机器人参考系、同设置固定相机实验，以及机器人搭载相机位姿保持（闭环视觉反馈）测试和摆落抓取试验。

Result: 固定相机实验中，三种布局将正面朝向平均误差从单平面标签的2.18°降至0.24–0.47°，位置RMSE从1.50 mm降至0.17–0.20 mm。搭载相机位姿保持测试确认了该分离效果。相同条件下的摆落试验中，两种带平接触条的布局抓取滑移约2 mm，无平接触条的布局滑移约100 mm。

Conclusion: 在所测条件下结果支持一条规则：位姿估计一致性优先时选用无平接触条布局；标记面需保持可抓取时选用带平接触条布局。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation of labware is difficult when transparent or reflective objects must be identified and localized. Coded planar fiducials are a practical retrofit: easy to print, they leave the marked face flat and graspable. Yet a single planar tag is least reliable in near-frontal views, where perspective cues fade. Non-planar geometries restore those cues but intrude on the flat face that a parallel-jaw gripper must contact. Our idea is to tilt multiple tags within one compact footprint, so that each tag is seen at a non-frontal angle even when the marker faces the camera. We propose the quARtet marker, a 3D-printable fiducial embodying this idea: all detected corners of its four tilted AprilTags enter one Perspective-n-Point solve, and a shared configuration defines the fabricated geometry and the detector model. Because tilting consumes flat area, its three layouts trade pose-estimation consistency against graspability. In robot-referenced, same-setup fixed-camera experiments, all three layouts reduced the mean frontal orientation error from 2.18 degree for a single planar tag to 0.24-0.47 degree and the root-mean-square position error from 1.50 to 0.17-0.20 mm. A robot-mounted-camera pose-hold test confirmed this separation under closed-loop visual feedback. In swing-down trials under identical conditions, the two layouts with flat contact strips retained the object with about 2 mm of in-grasp slip, whereas the layout without flat strips slipped by roughly 100 mm. For the tested conditions, the results support a rule: the layout without flat strips when pose-estimation consistency dominates, a layout with flat strips when the marked face must remain graspable.

</details>


### [214] [WBAG: A Whole-Body and Attached-Geometry Safety Framework for Vision-Language-Action Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01083)
*Samuel Zhen,Siwon Jo,Yanze Zhang,Wenhao Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出WBAG，一个在推理阶段对视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略施加安全约束的框架，通过建模机器人全身及随抓取变化的对象几何，并将其转化为可微CBF约束，以最小程度修改VLA的六维操作空间动作，从而避免机器人、场景与附着物体间的碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略在通用机器人操作上表现出色，但实际部署中仍面临机器人本体、被操作对象及周围环境之间发生碰撞的风险。现有推理阶段的安全框架多依赖以末端执行器为中心的简化表示，未显式建模完整的关节式机器人几何及附着物体的几何形状，导致安全防护不充分。

Method: WBAG构建随抓取条件变化的“安全集”，在对象被抓取时自适应更新受保护几何；随后将该不断演变的几何转化为可微控制障碍函数（CBF）约束，在推理阶段对VLA原生的六维操作空间动作进行最小修改，实现机器人、场景与附着几何之间的碰撞规避。

Result: 在SafeLIBERO基准（为安全性评估加入障碍物的LIBERO变体）上，采用监控所有非任务对象的场景级安全评估器，WBAG取得最佳综合安全性与安全任务成功率，达到97.38%的Scene Safety与59.38%的Safe Success。

Conclusion: 显式建模机器人全身及抓取相关附着几何，并将其嵌入可微CBF约束，可在不显著改变VLA策略原生动作的前提下有效提升操作安全性，为VLA在真实世界中的安全部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies have demonstrated impressive capabilities in generalizable robotic manipulation, but their deployment in the real world remains challenging due to potential collisions involving different parts of the robot, manipulated objects, and the surrounding environment. Existing inference-time VLA safety frameworks typically rely on simplified end-effector-centered representations that do not explicitly model the full articulated robot and attached-object geometry. In this paper, we present WBAG, a safety framework that models the robot's whole-body and grasp-dependent attached geometry. WBAG constructs a grasp-conditioned safe set that adapts the protected geometry as objects are grasped, then converts this evolving geometry into differentiable CBF constraints that minimally modify the VLA's native six-dimensional operational-space action for collision avoidance across robot, scene, and attached geometry. On the SafeLIBERO benchmark, a variant of LIBERO augmented with obstacles for safety evaluation, WBAG achieves the best overall safety and safe task success among the evaluated methods under a scene-level safety evaluator that monitors all eligible non-task objects, reaching 97.38\% aggregate Scene Safety and 59.38\% Safe Success.

</details>


### [215] [OrbitTAMP: Grounding Language Models for Task and Motion Planning in Spacecraft Rendezvous](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01093)
*Yuji Takubo,Daniele Gammelli,Marco Pavone,Simone D'Amico*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层框架，将LLM的语义推理与可复用行为图和领域规划模块相结合，实现航天器交会与近距离操作的任务与运动规划（TAMP），显著提升意图恢复的准确性与可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 航天器交会与近距离操作（RPO）目前依赖专家密集型流程，工程师需将高层操作意图转化为安全且动力学可行的轨迹，成为规模化操作的瓶颈。基于LLM的智能体虽能提供直观接口，但其输出并未天然地受轨道动力学、操作约束和可行机动结构的约束。

Method: 提出一个分层TAMP框架，将LLM推理基于可复用行为图和领域专用规划模块。预训练LLM将自然语言指令映射为部分任务规范；相关规划器在可容许操作空间内解决未指定决策；最后通过轨迹优化将完整任务规范转化为动力学可行轨迹。

Result: 实验表明该架构相比直接LLM生成大幅提升意图恢复能力，在所有评估划分上，当由前沿LLM支持时，部分任务规范精确恢复率达98%。测试时计算实验显示，对于紧凑的9B模型，验证器引导的修正将精确恢复率从75%提升至88%，更广的行为规划搜索也独立改善了可容许轨迹实现方案的选择。

Conclusion: 这些结果为语言驱动的航天器RPO智能体规划建立了可扩展且可审计的基础。

Abstract: Spacecraft rendezvous and proximity operations (RPO) are currently planned through an expertise-intensive process in which engineers translate high-level operational intent into safe, dynamically feasible trajectories, creating a bottleneck to scalable operations. Large language model (LLM)-based agents could offer an intuitive interface for this process, although their outputs are not inherently grounded in orbital dynamics, operational constraints, or the structure of admissible spacecraft maneuvers. To exploit their semantic reasoning while ensuring the generated plan's physical validity, this paper presents a hierarchical framework for spacecraft task-and-motion planning (TAMP) that grounds LLM reasoning in a graph of reusable behaviors and domain-specific planning modules. Within this framework, a pretrained LLM maps a natural-language command to a partial mission specification. The associated planners then resolve unspecified decisions within the admissible operational space. Finally, trajectory optimization converts the completed mission specification into a dynamically feasible trajectory. Numerical experiments demonstrate that this architecture substantially improves intent recovery over direct LLM generation, achieving 98% exact recovery of partial mission specifications across all evaluated splits when backed by frontier LLMs. Additional test-time-compute experiments show that, for a compact 9B model, verifier-guided revision increases exact recovery from 75% to 88%, while broader behavior-plan search independently improves selection among admissible trajectory realizations. Overall, these results establish a scalable and auditable foundation for language-driven agentic planning of spacecraft RPO.

</details>


### [216] [MASkillBlender: Decentralized Whole-Body Coordination for Multi-Humanoid Loco-Manipulation via Skill Blending](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01102)
*Yifan Hu,Luhang Hong,Mingkang Long,Danning Wang,Chengfeng Jia,Rong Su,Junjie Fu,Guanghui Wen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MASkillBlender，一个通用多智能体强化学习框架，通过复用预训练的单人形技能并学习共享的去中心化高层策略，实现多人的形全身协调，仅需任务级奖励。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多人的形协调运动操作面临高维全身控制、去中心化决策和可扩展性挑战。现有强化学习虽提升了单人的形全身控制，但扩展到多人形场景困难，往往需要大量奖励工程或任务特定设计。

Method: MASkillBlender在可复用的预训练单人的形技能之上学习共享的去中心化高层策略，仅用任务级奖励实现协调，无需任务特定运动参考。提出基于置换的数据增强策略用于同构多人形系统，并在同构马尔可夫博弈下从理论上证明置换样本保持原样本的策略梯度方向。

Result: 在两种人形本体上的多个多人形协调任务中评估，仿真结果表明该框架持续取得强任务性能，并在不同任务和人形本体上实现协调行为。

Conclusion: MASkillBlender为多人形全身协调提供了通用、去中心化且可扩展的强化学习方案，降低了对奖励工程和任务特定设计的依赖。

Abstract: Coordinated multi-humanoid loco-manipulation is promising yet challenging due to high-dimensional whole-body control, decentralized decision making, and scalability. While recent reinforcement learning methods have improved single-humanoid whole-body control, extending them to the multi-humanoid setting remains nontrivial and often requires substantial reward engineering or task-specific design. We propose MASkillBlender, a general multi-agent reinforcement learning framework to achieve decentralized multi-humanoid whole-body coordination. By learning a shared decentralized high-level policy over reusable pre-trained single-humanoid skills, MASkillBlender enables coordinated behaviors using only task-level rewards, without requiring task-specific motion references. To improve learning efficiency, we further introduce a permutation-based data augmentation strategy for homogeneous multi-humanoid systems, and theoretically show that the permuted samples preserve the policy-gradient direction of the original samples under the homogeneous Markov game formulation. We evaluate MASkillBlender on multiple multi-humanoid coordination tasks across two humanoid embodiments. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently achieves strong task performance and enables coordinated behaviors across different tasks and humanoid embodiments.

</details>


### [217] [Extreme Length Generalization in a Compact Recurrent Architecture for One-Shot Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01105)
*Izen Thornton,Aaron Shey,William Su*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FRANK：507K参数的循环架构，结合tau门控循环模块、内容可寻址记忆与前馈反射通路，在远超训练长度的超长序列上保持高精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人在一次性任务中运行的时间跨度远超训练时所见轨迹，且机载算力固定，现有循环、状态空间等架构难以在极长序列下维持精度。

Method: 提出FRANK，507K参数循环架构，含tau门控循环模块、内容可寻址记忆、前馈反射通路；与循环、状态空间、精简模块化基线在相同参数量下对比，在四个算法序列任务上训练长度5-20、评测至两百万token；并进行组件消融（lesion）分析。

Result: 在10万倍最大训练长度处，10个FRANK种子中6个保持100.0%准确率，50个基线配置无一达到（Fisher精确检验，双侧p=4.2E-6）；消融得到四种不同的组件依赖模式；仿真训练的FRANK策略可驱动物理地面车辆无遥操作地穿越障碍到达指令航点。

Conclusion: FRANK通过任务相关的循环、记忆与反射通路分配，在超长时域上实现稳健泛化，并具备迁移到真实机器人导航的潜力。

Abstract: Autonomous robots on one-shot missions run over horizons far longer than the trajectories seen during training, under a fixed onboard compute budget. We present FRANK, a 507K-parameter recurrent architecture that combines tau-gated recurrent modules, content-addressable memory, and a feedforward reflex pathway. We evaluate it against recurrent, state-space, and reduced modular baselines at matched parameter count on four algorithmic sequence tasks, trained at length 5-20 and evaluated out to two million tokens. At 100,000x the maximum training length, 6 of 10 FRANK seeds retain exactly 100.0% accuracy, while none of the 50 baseline configurations does, five architectures at ten seeds each with none left incomplete (Fisher exact, two-sided p = 4.2E-6. Targeted lesions across the four tasks yield four distinct component-reliance profiles, consistent with task-dependent allocation across the recurrent, memory, and reflex pathways. Separately, a FRANK policy trained in simulation drives a physical ground vehicle to commanded waypoints through obstacles without teleoperation.

</details>


### [218] [Experience-Based Feasibility-Aware Generative Adversarial Imitation from Observation under Embodiment Mismatch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01171)
*Yoshiki Takebayashi,Giovanni Perantoni,Hikaru Sasaki,Matteo Saveriano,Takamitsu Matsubara*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EF-GAIfO方法，利用机器人自身经验动态估计无动作标签演示的可行性，实现随策略学习演化的可行域扩展式观察模仿学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无机器人演示界面提供无动作标签的状态轨迹，使观察模仿学习成为从人类演示学习机器人行为的有前景方法。但人与机器人在具身结构与动力学上存在差异，人类演示动作可能对机器人不可行，从而降低策略性能。

Method: EF-GAIfO（基于经验的可行性感知生成对抗观察模仿）不依赖显式动力学模型或大规模先验探索数据集，而是从机器人自身经验中估计纯状态演示的可行性。其核心特点是可行性概念随策略学习而演化：策略改进、机器人经历更广状态转移后，可行区域逐步扩展，使更多演示可被纳入学习，从而实现适应策略学习阶段的可行性感知模仿。

Result: 在仿真中的运动任务以及真实四足机器人执行物体可达并抓取任务上验证了EF-GAIfO的有效性。

Conclusion: EF-GAIfO通过基于机器人自身经验、随策略学习动态扩展可行域的方式，有效缓解了人类演示可行性不足的问题，在仿真与真实机器人任务中均表现出有效性。

Abstract: With the increasing use of robot-free demonstration interfaces that provide state trajectories without action labels, imitation from observation has become a promising approach for learning robot behaviors from human demonstrations. However, due to differences in embodiment and dynamics between humans and robots, demonstrated human motions may not be feasible for the robot, potentially degrading policy performance. In this study, we propose Experience-Based Feasibility-Aware Generative Adversarial Imitation from Observation (EF-GAIfO), which estimates the feasibility of state-only demonstrations from the robot's own experience rather than relying on explicit dynamics models or large prior exploration datasets. A key feature of EF-GAIfO is that the notion of feasibility evolves with policy learning: as the policy improves and the robot experiences a broader range of state transitions, the feasible region is progressively expanded, allowing additional demonstrations to be incorporated into learning. This enables feasibility-aware imitation that adapts to the current stage of policy learning, rather than relying on a pre-designed feasibility criterion. We validate the effectiveness of EF-GAIfO on a locomotion task in simulation and on a real quadruped robot performing a object-reaching-and-grasping task.

</details>


### [219] [Recova: Agent-Guided Failure Recovery for Autonomous Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01178)
*Isabella Liu,An-Chieh Cheng,Johan Bjorck,Zhiding Yu,Hongxu Yin,Jan Kautz,Linxi Fan,Yuke Zhu,Sifei Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Recova 是一个智能体引导的框架，在数字孪生中联合训练任务执行与恢复策略，并通过真机经验验证精炼，将失败转化为可复用能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 操作失败会使场景进入任务策略无法恢复的状态，而学习纠正行为需要可扩展的失败探索与物理基础。现有方法缺乏系统化的失败探索与恢复学习机制。

Method: 在重建的数字孪生中，智能体诊断失败、测试纠正程序，并分别收集任务与恢复的成功轨迹训练独立策略。部署时监控进度、调用学习型或程序化恢复、验证场景恢复后继续执行。无可用恢复时由人工演示解决并进入学习循环，真机轨迹与人工演示按策略路由做 DAgger 训练。

Result: 在六个 LIBERO-Pro 设置和四个 MolmoSpaces 类别上，Recova 平均成功率为 78.8% 和 64.9%，基线最强为 71.7% 和 38.0%。四台真机工作站的并行采集下，DAgger 微调将平均成功率从 23.8% 提升到 77.5%，恢复技能进一步提升至 87.5%。单个任务四轮采集中，人工干预从 87.5% 降至 0%。

Conclusion: 智能体引导的恢复机制能够将失败转化为可复用能力，在提升鲁棒性的同时逐步减少人工干预。

Abstract: Manipulation failures can leave scenes in states from which a task policy cannot recover. Learning corrective behaviors requires scalable failure exploration and physical grounding. We present Recova, an agent-guided framework that jointly develops task execution and recovery in a reconstructed digital twin, then verifies and refines both through real-world experience. In the twin, the agent diagnoses failures, tests corrective programs, and collects successful task and recovery rollouts for separate policies. During deployment, it monitors progress, invokes a learned or programmatic recovery, verifies scene restoration, and resumes execution. When no suitable recovery is available, a human demonstration resolves the failure and enters the learning loop, allowing the system to expand its recovery capabilities. Physical rollouts and human demonstrations are routed to the corresponding policy for DAgger training. Across six LIBERO-Pro settings and four MolmoSpaces categories, Recova achieves 78.8% and 64.9% mean success, compared with 71.7% and 38.0% for the strongest baselines. With parallel collection across four real-robot workstations, DAgger fine-tuning raises mean success from 23.8% to 77.5%, and recovery skills further raise it to 87.5%. Over four collection rounds on one task, observed human intervention falls from 87.5% to 0%. Together, these results show how agent-guided recovery turns failures into reusable capabilities, improving robustness while progressively reducing human intervention. Project page: https://www.liuisabella.com/Recova

</details>


### [220] [AFD-CAMLs: Agile Force-Distribution-Aware Planning and Control for Cable-Suspended Aerial Multi-Lifting Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01185)
*Antreas Kourris,Sihao Sun*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出规划与控制混合框架，解决多无人机协同吊运中力分配不均问题，在保持机动性的同时实现更均衡的张力分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多无人机协同吊运重物时需同时控制位置与姿态。基于轨迹的方法机动性高且满足系统约束，但在张力-力旋量分配冗余或病态时（尤其几何失配与底层跟踪误差下）会产生不均的力分布。

Method: 提出混合规划与控制框架：全局规划器在给定内力设定下探索分配零空间，生成负载轨迹与缆绳力参考；这些参考被加入集中式局部规划器的代价中，在生成所有无人机轨迹的同时促进可行力分布；随后用导纳滤波器比较规划力与机载缆绳张力估计，调整运动学参考以改善退化或近退化构型下的力跟踪。

Result: 在四至十架无人机的仿真与实验中，悬停及高难度敏捷机动过程中均实现了更均衡的张力分布，且未牺牲机动性与负载跟踪性能。

Conclusion: 该框架通过结合全局力分配优化、局部规划代价增强与导纳滤波，有效改善了多无人机协同吊运中的力分布均衡性。

Abstract: Multiple UAVs can cooperatively transport heavy payloads while controlling their position and orientation. Trajectory-based methods offer high agility while satisfying system constraints, but can produce uneven force distributions when the tension-to-wrench allocation is redundant or ill-conditioned, particularly under geometric mismatch and low-level tracking errors. We propose a hybrid planning-and-control framework to address this problem. A global planner generates payload trajectories and cable-force references by exploring the allocation null space under a prescribed internal-force setting. These references augment the cost of a centralized local planner, promoting feasible force distributions while generating trajectories for all UAVs. An admittance filter then compares the planned forces with onboard cable-tension estimates and adjusts the kinematic references to improve force tracking in degenerate or near-degenerate configurations. Simulations and experiments involving four to ten UAVs demonstrate more balanced tension distributions during both hovering and demanding agile maneuvers, without compromising agility or payload-tracking performance.

</details>


### [221] [EIDA: Execution-Interface Dynamics Adaptation for Real-to-Sim-to-Real Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01219)
*Yiwei Qian,Shanze Wang,Qingyuan Hu,Xinming Zhang,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出执行接口动力学自适应（EIDA），从目标平台数据拟合速度指令响应，提升仿真到真机的导航迁移效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仿真到机器人迁移时，速度指令产生的运动与反馈和训练时建模不一致，导致迁移失败；而重建执行器动力学复杂。

Method: EIDA 不重建执行器动力学，而是拟合目标平台执行数据：一个模型用体坐标系位姿增量更新仿真几何，另一个模型预测策略观测到的速度反馈，并将速度反馈短历史输入策略；拟合模型用于轻量 GPU 并行仿真器。

Result: 在 Jackal 与 Go2 验证集上降低位置和偏航预测误差；在100个基准导航环境中取得最高成功率和导航分数；反馈消融验证了匹配策略侧速度估计的必要性；真实 Unitree Go2 静态场景20次全部无碰撞到达，基线仅4次。

Conclusion: 执行接口自适应无需详细执行器仿真即可改善导航迁移性能。

Abstract: Simulation-to-robot transfer can fail when velocity commands produce motion and feedback that differ from those modeled during policy training. We present execution-interface dynamics adaptation (EIDA), which fits these responses from target-platform execution data without reconstructing actuator dynamics. A model of body-frame pose increments updates simulator geometry, while a separate model predicts the velocity feedback observed by the policy; a short history of velocity feedback is included in the policy input. The fitted models are used within a lightweight GPU-parallel simulator. On the full Jackal and Go2 validation sets, the fitted models reduced position and yaw prediction errors relative to the simulator's predefined motion model. Across 100 benchmark navigation environments evaluated in a separate physics-based simulator, EIDA achieved the highest success rate and navigation score among the compared learned policies, both with and without global guidance. Feedback ablations further supported the need to match policy-facing velocity estimates. On a physical Unitree Go2, EIDA reached the goal without collision in all 20 static-scene trials, compared with 4 of 20 for the baseline. These results show that execution-interface adaptation can improve navigation transfer without detailed actuator simulation.

</details>


### [222] [ColoACT: Multi-Cue Action Chunking for Smooth Autonomous Colon Navigation on a Self-Propelled Endoscopic Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01258)
*Jian Hu,Shujing He,Leixin Chang,Zongze Li,Ding Huang,Chaoyang Shi,Chengzhi Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ColoACT系统，融合RGB-D-E动作分块Transformer策略与自驱动齿轮内镜机器人BGER，实现结肠镜自主导航，在体外猪结肠中取得较高成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主结肠镜导航可减轻操作者负担并降低成环或组织损伤风险，但面临可变形解剖结构、弱纹理与镜面高光视觉、接触丰富的黏弹性交互等挑战。现有几何驱动方法高效可解释但脆弱，学习型策略则存在时序不一致、过度平滑及仿真到现实差距等问题。

Method: 提出ColoACT，将RGB-D-E动作分块Transformer策略与紧凑自驱动斜齿轮内镜机器人BGER结合。策略在RGB基础上引入估计相对深度和基于梯度的伪高程图，增强褶皱脊线等高频道几何线索；通过预测重叠动作块并经时间集成融合，实现BGER的平滑连续控制。

Result: 在约60厘米的体外猪结肠中，直线段成功率85.4%，弯曲段72.5%；90度转弯成功率70%，双弯序列60%，并在三弯段验证了可行性。

Conclusion: ColoACT通过融合深度与伪高程线索及动作分块时序集成，提升了复杂结肠环境下的自主导航鲁棒性与成功率，验证了其在多弯段场景中的可行性。

Abstract: Autonomous colonoscopic navigation can reduce operator burden and the risk of loop formation or tissue trauma, but remains challenging due to deformable anatomy, weak-texture and specular endoscopic visuals, and contact-rich viscoelastic interactions. Existing methods either rely on geometry-driven pipelines, which are efficient and interpretable yet brittle due to manually engineered features and switching logic, or adopt learning-based policies, whose inferred depth/geometry can become temporally inconsistent or overly smooth under weak texture and specular highlights while simulation-trained variants (e.g., deep reinforcement learning) may further suffer from a sim-to-real gap. We propose ColoACT, an autonomous navigation system that integrates an RGB-D-E based Action Chunking Transformer policy (ColoACT policy) for a compact self-propelled Bevel-Gear-Based Endoscopic Robot (BGER). The ColoACT policy augments RGB with estimated relative depth and a gradient-based pseudo-elevation map to enhance fold-ridge saliency and other high-frequency geometric cues, and enables smooth continuous control of the BGER by predicting overlapping action chunks and fusing them via temporal ensembling. In different \textit{ex-vivo} porcine colons (approximately 60 cm), our system achieves success rates of 85.4\% and 72.5\% in straight and curved segments, respectively, and achieves 70\% success in 90-degree turns and 60\% in double-bend sequences, with feasibility further demonstrated in challenging triple-bend segments. The project page is available at: https://Adamhu1.github.io/ColoACT/.

</details>


### [223] [PROMO: Preference-conditioned Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning for Quadrupedal Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01260)
*Amr Mousa,Rifny Rachman,Neil Karavis,Michele Caprio,Richard Allmendinger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PROMO 提出一种语义多目标强化学习方法，将四足运动的权衡作为运行时输入，用单一策略实现偏好条件化控制，并在仿真与真机上验证了帕累托覆盖与零样本迁移性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足运动需要同时平衡指令跟踪、稳定性与能效等相互冲突的目标，但传统强化学习在训练时就把这些优先级硬编码为固定标量奖励，无法在部署时灵活调整权衡。

Method: PROMO 将权衡转换为策略的显式运行时输入：以面向部署的偏好对策略进行条件化，同时保持与具体机器人本体相关的运动先验固定，从而把操作者意图与生成可行步态所需的奖励整形项分离。

Result: 在仿真中采样 100 个偏好，67 个行为在精确帕累托支配下非受支配，偏好与目标的平均相关性为 0.843；同一策略零样本迁移到 Unitree Go2，仅改变偏好即使比能耗降低最多 30.4%、位置误差降低 38.7%、机身姿态峰值偏差降低 59.0%。

Conclusion: 偏好条件化多目标强化学习可作为自适应腿足运动的实用运行时接口，将其作用从离线帕累托集构建扩展到在线部署阶段。

Abstract: Quadrupedal locomotion requires balancing conflicting objectives such as command tracking, stability, and energy efficiency, yet conventional reinforcement learning (RL) hardcodes these priorities into a fixed scalar reward at training time. We present PROMO (Preference-Conditioned Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning), a semantic multi-objective approach that makes this trade-off an explicit runtime input to a single locomotion policy. PROMO conditions the policy on deployment facing preferences while keeping embodiment-specific locomotion priors fixed, thereby separating operator intent from reward shaping terms required for viable gait generation. Compared with fixed-objective controllers, multi-objective baselines, and independently trained specialists, PROMO achieves objective specialization and robustness from a single deployable policy. Across 100 sampled preferences in simulation, 67 behaviors are non-dominated under exact Pareto dominance, with a mean preference-objective correlation of 0.843, demonstrating broad Pareto coverage and predictable preference response. The same policy transfers zero-shot to a Unitree Go2, where preference changes alone reduce specific energy by up to 30.4%, position error by 38.7%, and peak body-attitude deviation by 59.0% relative to the balanced preference. These results establish preference-conditioned multi-objective RL as a practical runtime interface for adaptive legged locomotion, extending its role beyond offline Pareto-set construction. Open-source code and videos are available at https://amrmousa.com/promo/.

</details>


### [224] [Interaction-Stiffness-Guided Basis Allocation in Dynamic Movement Primitives for Efficient Skill Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01288)
*Chan Xu,Silu Chen,Dehao Wang,Xiyu Chen,Dexin Jiang,Chi Zhang,Guilin Yang,Chenguang Yang,Zaojun Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SC-DMP，用阶段关键性指标引导基函数自适应分配，在高关键性阶段密集表达，保持模型紧凑与经典DMP稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典DMP基函数布局预先固定，无法按各阶段不同的精度需求分配逼近能力，导致高精度阶段表示能力不足。

Method: 融合操作者-机器人交互刚度与跨示教任务空间变异性导出的轨迹一致性线索，构建阶段关键性指标；据此通过逆累积关键性在归一化时间上重新分布基中心并映射到规范相位域，同时细化带宽以调整局部逼近支撑。

Result: 在书写轨迹与三个真实机器人任务上，推断的关键性集中于几何要求高、任务约束强的区域；与DMP、ProMP、ProDMP、GP-MP、KMP相比，轨迹复现、端点泛化与任务关键精度均提升，同时保持紧凑模型与经典DMP稳定结构。

Conclusion: SC-DMP通过关键性引导的基函数自适应分配，在保持经典DMP紧凑稳定优势的同时提升了精度关键技能学习性能。

Abstract: Dynamic Movement Primitives (DMPs) provide a compact and stable formulation for trajectory representation and generalization in robot skill learning. However, their predefined basis layout limits the allocation of approximation capacity according to stage-dependent precision requirements. To address this issue, this article proposes Stage-Criticality-Guided Dynamic Movement Primitives (SC-DMPs) with adaptive basis allocation for precision-critical skill learning. Operator-robot interaction stiffness and a trajectory-consistency cue derived from cross-demonstration task-space variability are integrated to construct a stage-criticality index. Guided by this index, basis centers are redistributed in normalized time through inverse cumulative criticality and mapped to the canonical phase domain, while their bandwidths are refined to adjust local approximation support. This enables denser and more flexible representation at high-criticality stages while retaining sparser allocation elsewhere. Experiments on handwriting trajectories and three real-robot tasks show that the inferred criticality is concentrated in geometrically demanding and task-constrained regions. Comparisons with DMPs, ProMPs, ProDMP, GP-MP, and KMP demonstrate improved trajectory reproduction, endpoint generalization, and task-critical accuracy while retaining a compact model and the stable structure of classical DMPs.

</details>


### [225] [Continual Learning for 6-DoF Grasp Synthesis via Experience and Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01301)
*Giulio Schiavi,Andrei Cramariuc,Michael Pantic,Roland Siegwart*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于杂乱场景下单视角6-DoF抓取合成的持续学习框架，通过嵌入空间中的记忆而非微调大模型来实现在线适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有抓取合成系统多为离线训练且部署后保持固定，当部署条件（如陌生物体）偏离训练数据时性能会下降。

Method: 不微调大型参数模型，而是通过学习到的嵌入空间中的记忆进行适应：抓取结果更新未来的抓取评分，同时可选地回忆用户示范并将其迁移到新场景作为额外候选抓取。

Result: 在仿真和超过1500次真实世界抓取试验中评估：在适应前即可匹配现有6-DoF抓取基线；对训练中缺失或欠代表类别的陌生物体可在线改进；支持长时程持续学习且遗忘有限；真实实验中挑战性类别仅需50次在线抓取即可达90%以上成功率。

Conclusion: 该持续学习框架能有效提升机器人在未见物体和场景下的6-DoF抓取能力，并具备较低的遗忘与良好的长时程适应性。

Abstract: Most current grasp synthesis systems are trained offline and remain fixed during deployment. While this works well when deployment conditions resemble the training data, performance can degrade when robots encounter conditions they have not seen before, such as unfamiliar objects. In this work, we present a continual-learning framework for single-view 6-DoF grasp synthesis for a parallel-jaw gripper in cluttered scenes. Rather than finetuning a large parametric model, our method adapts through memory in a learned embedding space: grasp outcomes update future grasp scores, while optional user demonstrations are recalled and transferred to new scenes as additional candidate grasps. We evaluate our method in simulation and in extensive real-world experiments comprising over 1500 grasp trials. We show that our method matches the performance of existing 6-DoF grasping baselines even before adaptation, improves online on unseen objects from categories absent or underrepresented during training, and supports long-horizon continual learning with limited forgetting. In real-world experiments, our method reaches over 90\% success rates on several challenging object categories after only 50 online grasp attempts. Videos and code at https://giuschio.github.io/cl_grasping/.

</details>


### [226] [Ex vivo breach detection using electrical conductivity during robotic pedicle drilling in the spine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01310)
*Jorge Andrés Pérez Velásquez,François Teyssere,Thibault Chandanson,Quentin Grimal,Brahim Tamadazte*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种结合SpineGuard本体感觉电阻抗传感器的机器人椎弓根钻孔方法，通过实时分析电阻抗信号检测穿孔风险，无需外部传感器即可预防突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 椎弓根螺钉置入在脊柱侧弯治疗中技术难度高，因视野受限、解剖变异和并发症风险而需要高精度。现有机器人系统虽辅助CT规划与执行，但仍依赖术中电离辐射成像和复杂配准，因此需要一种不依赖外部成像和传感器的实时穿孔预防方案。

Method: 开发机器人钻孔方法，配备SpineGuard的本体感觉电阻抗传感器工具；设计实时检测算法，在钻孔过程中分析电阻抗信号，识别与潜在突破至椎管相关的电导率突变；该方法仅依赖工具自身信号，无需外部设备或传感器。

Result: 离体实验中，该方法在全部51例钻孔中成功预防了突破（摘要中同时出现"51例中预防50例"的表述，但最终明确为全部51例均成功预防），表明系统能在钻孔中检测危险事件并在发生前停止操作。

Conclusion: 本工作证明了结合电阻抗传感的机器人椎弓根钻孔用于实时穿孔预防的可行性。仅使用工具信号即可消除对外部传感系统的需求，支持更安全的椎弓根螺钉置入。

Abstract: Purpose: Pedicle screw placement is technically demanding in scoliosis treatment. High precision is required due to limited visibility, anatomical variability, and the risk of complications. Although robotic systems assist CT-based planning and execution, they still rely on ionizing intraoperative imaging and complex registration. This study proposes robotic pedicle drilling with real-time preventive breach detection using electrical bioimpedance sensing.
  Methods: We developed a robotic approach combined with a pedicle-drilling tool equipped with a proprioceptive electrical bioimpedance sensor developed by SpineGuard. A real-time detection algorithm was designed to analyze the electrical bioimpedance signal during drilling and identify abrupt changes in conductivity associated with potential breaches towards the spinal canal. The method operates without external devices or sensors.
  Results: The ex vivo experiments showed that the proposed method prevented breaches in 100 of the 51 drilling cases. These findings demonstrate the system's ability to detect potentially hazardous events during drilling and to stop the procedure before. The ex vivo experiments demonstrated that the proposed method prevented breaches in all 51 drilling cases.
  Conclusions: This work demonstrates the feasibility of robotic pedicle drilling with electrical bioimpedance sensing for real-time breach prevention. Using only the tool signal, the method eliminates the need for external sensing systems and supports safer pedicle screw placement.

</details>


### [227] [Cross-entropy optimization with prioritized constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01319)
*Francisco Roldan Sanchez,Pau de las Heras Molins,David Fridovich-Keil,Georgios Bakirtzis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TierCEM 将严格的约束优先级直接嵌入交叉熵方法的精英选择过程，无需为每个约束设置重要性权重，从而在约束冲突时按优先级依次保留高优先级约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在约束冲突时，基于优先级排序的方法规定哪些需求优先，而基于惩罚的方法通过数值权重编码相对重要性。但依赖权重时，解可能提高加权得分却违反预定优先级。因此需要一个不依赖逐约束权重、又能严格遵守优先级顺序的优化方法。

Method: 提出 TierCEM，一种交叉熵方法的变体。它按约束优先级从高到低依次过滤采样候选者；当某个约束淘汰所有剩余候选者时，返回上一个非空候选集合，并选择对该阻塞约束违反最小的精英，递归地保持所有更高优先级约束的满足。

Result: 在2D导航和接触丰富的推动任务中，于本体感知和学习世界模型两种设置下评估。实验表明，颠倒约束顺序会改变冲突下被违反的约束；优先考虑任务目标的进展还能让 TierCEM 在低优先级约束阻碍进一步进展时放松这些约束。

Conclusion: TierCEM 通过将严格约束优先级直接纳入精英选择，避免了逐约束权重需求，并在约束冲突场景中按优先级有效决定约束的保留与放松。

Abstract: When constraints conflict, an optimizer must determine which requirements to preserve and which to relax. On the one hand, a priority ordering specifies which requirements take precedence. On the other hand, penalty-based formulations encode their relative importance through numerical weights. Depending on these weights, a solution can improve its weighted score while violating intended priorities. We introduce TierCEM, a variant of the cross-entropy method that incorporates strict constraint priorities directly into elite selection without requiring per-constraint importance weights. TierCEM works by sequentially filtering sampled candidates, from highest- to lowest-priority constraint. If and when a constraint eliminates all remaining candidates, TierCEM returns to the last nonempty set and selects elites with the smallest violations of that blocking constraint, recursively preserving satisfaction of all higher-priority constraints. We evaluate TierCEM on 2D navigation and contact-rich pushing tasks in proprioceptive and learned world-model settings. Experiments show that reversing the constraint ordering changes which constraints are violated under conflict. Prioritizing progress toward the task objective also enables TierCEM to relax lower-priority constraints when they would otherwise prevent further progress.

</details>


### [228] [Is Success All You Need? Investigating the Impact of Input Perturbations on VLA Behaviour in Tabletop Manipulation Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01351)
*Sophie Higham,Riccardo Andrea Izzo,Matteo Matteucci,Alessandro Suglia*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个与基准无关的评估框架，通过刻画扰动下成功轨迹的执行方式来衡量VLA模型的行为鲁棒性，并发现仅靠任务成功率(TSR)无法反映行为层面的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型鲁棒性评估主要依赖任务成功率(TSR)，但TSR只能说明任务是否完成，无法反映机器人在扰动下"如何完成"任务，因而可能掩盖成功轨迹行为的显著变化。

Method: 提出与基准无关的行为鲁棒性评估框架，扩展LIBERO与LIBERO-Plus基准，对三种先进VLA模型、四个LIBERO任务套件和七种扰动条件进行评估，测量典型成功行为及其变异性的变化，包括运动平滑度、效率和夹爪行为等指标。

Result: 扰动会改变成功轨迹的行为，这一现象无法仅从TSR推断；在不同LIBERO套件中，存在先进VLA模型在相同扰动下TSR相当、但成功轨迹行为差异显著的情况。

Conclusion: 评估VLA模型鲁棒性时，除了是否完成任务，还应关注机器人完成任务的执行方式；TSR可由刻画成功任务执行性质与变异性的行为评估指标加以补充。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have achieved high task success rates on robot manipulation task benchmarks. More recently, there has been an emphasis on evaluating the robustness of VLA models to perturbations. However, this robustness is still predominantly measured through Task Success Rate (TSR). In this work, we propose a benchmark-agnostic evaluation framework to measure the behavioural robustness of models by characterising how successful trajectories are executed under perturbation. We implement this methodology by extending the widely-used LIBERO and LIBERO-Plus benchmarks. Across three state-of-the-art VLA models, four LIBERO task suites and seven perturbation conditions, we evaluate changes in both typical successful behaviour and its variability, including metrics of motion smoothness, efficiency and gripper behaviour. We find that perturbations can alter the behaviour of successful trajectories, a phenomenon which cannot necessarily be inferred from TSR alone. Across LIBERO suites, we identify cases where state-of-the-art VLA models achieve comparable TSR under the same perturbation condition, yet behaviour on successful trajectories diverges substantially. Therefore, to have a more robust assessment of task performance, we argue that suitable measures of robustness should capture not only whether a task is completed, but also how the robot behaves while completing it. When evaluating the robustness of VLA models, TSR may be complemented by behavioural evaluation metrics that characterise the nature and variability of successful task execution by robots.

</details>


### [229] [Continue, Abort, or Fall: Viability-Aware Policy Selection (VAPS) for Safe Humanoid Acrobatics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01397)
*Siwei Ju,Lu Liu,Jan Peters,Oleg Arenz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出VAPS（可行性感知策略选择），将安全视为策略条件下的滚动时域决策，在跟踪、中止、保护性摔倒三类策略间按最小牺牲层级切换，显著降低人形机器人翻转动作为的头部/手部碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态人形动作（如空翻）在策略次优、扰动或仿真到现实差距下易损坏硬件；纯运动跟踪策略一旦偏离参考轨迹便无退路，需要备用策略接管以实现最小损伤落地，而"用哪个备用策略"与"何时切换"同样关键。

Method: VAPS包含保护性摔倒策略与可随时中止并双脚落地的高难度abort策略；每个控制步用学习到的预测器估计名义跟踪策略与abort策略在短时域内是否仍可行，并按最小牺牲层级保留仍可行且任务野心最大的行为。

Result: 在随机扰动仿真中，于Unitree G1与LimX Oli上大幅减少头部接触与手部接触；在LimX Oli上验证了可行性预测器及完整VAPS控制器（侧翻动作）。VAPS在任务成功率与头部冲击上帕累托优于最强单网络基线（端到端安全跟踪策略、从VAPS自身oracle路由决策蒸馏的学生策略）。

Conclusion: VAPS可作为监督欠训练策略、保护硬件的有效框架，且优于各类单网络替代方案。

Abstract: Dynamic humanoid motions such as flips risk hardware damage due to suboptimal policies, disturbances or sim-to-real gaps. A motion tracking policy offers no way out once the maneuver leaves its reference, and a backup policy needs to take over to protect the hardware for a minimum-damage landing. Which backup to use matters as much as when to switch. We present Viability-Aware Policy Selection (VAPS), which treats safety as a policy-conditioned, receding-horizon decision. Besides a protective fall policy, we also train an abort policy which can abort the motion at any time, landing on its feet. At every control step, learned predictors estimate whether the nominal tracking policy and the abort policy remain viable over a short horizon, and a least-sacrificial hierarchy keeps the most task-ambitious behavior that remains viable. In simulation with randomized disturbances, VAPS sharply reduces head contact and hand contact, which are the dominant sources of hardware damage, with both a Unitree G1 and a LimX Oli; on the LimX Oli, we validate the viability predictors and the full VAPS controller for side-flip motions. VAPS Pareto-dominates the strongest single-network alternatives we could train, including an end-to-end safe-tracking policy and students distilled from VAPS's own oracle-routed decisions, in both task success and head impact. We also show that VAPS is a powerful framework to supervise undertrained policies and protect the hardware.

</details>


### [230] [ALFRED: Requirement-driven development of an open-source mobile manipulator for long-term plant monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01477)
*Ciarán Miceal Johnson,Christopher Quail,Garry Ellard,Alistair McConnell,Steve Tonneau,Fernando Auat Cheein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ALFRED，一台开源移动操作机器人，通过四次迭代构建满足重复户外部署的六项要求，完成了一年528次森林月度调查，并量化了结构设计对机械臂可达性和LiDAR视野的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跟踪作物和森林的季节性变化需要反复观测同一批植物，地面机器人可近距离作业，机械臂能提供更多视角。但长期野外数据集背后的机器人很少公开设计文件，且机器人自身结构对机械臂可达范围和传感器遮挡的限制也很少在不同构建版本间进行比较。

Method: 提出开源移动操作机器人ALFRED，采用商用组件构建：Ackermann转向底盘上搭载六自由度机械臂、LiDAR、RGB-D相机、RTK GNSS和IMU，安装在可重构铝型材框架上，运行容器化ROS软件。围绕重复户外部署的六项要求（耐用性、模块化、可维修性、感知可达性、续航和可复现性），经过四次构建迭代开发，并对后三次构建进行基于模型的分析：计算机械臂可达位姿的可用比例，并用光线投射分析框架安装式LiDAR的水平视野遮挡情况。

Result: 基于模型的分析显示，后三次构建中机械臂可达位姿的可用比例从34.0%提升到60.0%，再到66.1%；光线投射表明只有最终构建能保持框架安装式LiDAR前后方向水平视野清晰。ALFRED完成了一年每月一次、共528次穿越的森林调查，未错过任何一次计划采集，期间经历了电池退化、为其他研究者研究而进行的重新配置，以及为自主作物行作业并行开发的ALFRED 2.0，每次构建切换约需六小时。

Conclusion: 长期野外部署验证了ALFRED的可靠性，同时表明机械模块化能否可靠运行，取决于跟踪其状态描述的机器人模型（robot description）是否同样可靠。

Abstract: Tracking seasonal change in crops and forests requires observing the same plants repeatedly. Ground robots can do this at close range, and a manipulator gives their sensors more viewpoints. Yet the robots behind long-term field datasets are rarely released with their design files, and how a robot's own structure limits arm reach and occludes its sensors is seldom compared between builds. We present ALFRED, an open-source mobile manipulator built from commercially available components. It carries a six-degree-of-freedom arm, LiDAR, RGB-D cameras, RTK GNSS and an IMU on an Ackermann-steered base, all mounted on a reconfigurable aluminium strut frame, and runs containerised ROS software. It was developed through four builds against six requirements for repeated outdoor deployment: durability, modularity, repairability, sensing reach, endurance and reproducibility. Model-based analysis of the last three builds shows the usable share of the arm's reachable poses rising from 34.0% to 60.0% and then 66.1%, and ray casting shows that only the final build keeps the frame-mounted LiDAR's horizontal view clear both forwards and backwards. ALFRED completed a year of monthly forest surveys (528 traversals) without missing a scheduled collection. This was despite battery degradation, reconfiguration for another researcher's study, and the parallel development of ALFRED 2.0 for autonomous crop-row operation, with each switch between builds taking about six hours. The deployment also showed that mechanical modularity is only as dependable as the robot description that tracks it.

</details>


### [231] [OpenSpace Lab Solution to the IROS 2026 Indoor Exploration Competition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01505)
*Yuxuan Zhang,Dong Li,Zezhou Sun,Yuxuan Xu,Siyu Teng,Yuchen Li,Jianjian Yang,Long Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文报告了 OpenSpace Lab 在 IROS 2026 单/多机器人智能信息收集竞赛中的解决方案，单机器人赛道采用预训练地图补全预测与剩余时间探索策略，多机器人赛道采用效用驱动的目标选择策略，最终获得单机器人公开赛道第 1 名及两个私有赛道第 3 名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限作业时间内实现高效的环境探索与地图覆盖，是单机器人与多机器人系统的核心挑战；单机器人需在覆盖与返航约束间权衡，多机器人需消除冗余搜索以提升协调效率。

Method: 单机器人框架利用预训练的地图补全预测进行全局规划，优先探索未探索区域，并引入基于剩余时间的探索策略，将返航约束融入决策过程；多机器人探索采用效用驱动的目标选择策略，权衡观测收益、移动代价与预算约束，并共享地图与意图数据。

Result: 单机器人公开赛道覆盖率达 61.04%；单机器人与多机器人私有赛道覆盖率分别为 39.53% 和 39.91%。团队获得单机器人公开赛道第 1 名，单/多机器人私有赛道均获第 3 名。

Conclusion: 该方案在单/多机器人探索任务中表现优异，验证了地图补全预测、剩余时间探索与效用驱动协调策略的有效性；基于本报告的完整论文正在准备投稿，源代码将在全文被接收后于 GitHub 发布。

Abstract: This report presents the \textbf{OpenSpace Lab}'s solution to the Competition on Intelligent Information Gathering for Single and Multi-Robot Systems Workshops, organized as part of the IEEE/RSJ International Conference on Intelligent Robots and Systems (IROS) 2026. Our team reached 1st place in the Single-Robot Public Track and 3rd place in both the Single- and Multi-Robot Private Tracks. The single-robot framework utilizes pre-trained map completion predictions for global planning to prioritize unexplored areas. To reconcile map coverage with limited operation time, we introduce a remaining-time-based exploration strategy that integrates homing constraints into the decision-making process. For multi-robot exploration, we utilize a utility-driven target selection strategy that balances observation gains, movement costs, and budget constraints, leveraging shared map and intent data to eliminate redundant search and maximize coordination efficiency. Our solution reached a 61.04\% coverage rate in the Single-Robot Public Track, while reaching 39.53\% and 39.91\% coverage in the Single- and Multi-Robot Private Tracks, respectively. An extended full-length paper based on this report is currently being prepared for submission, and the source code will be released upon acceptance of the full manuscript at https://github.com/OpenSpace-Lab/Indoor-Exploration-IROS2026.

</details>


### [232] [Completion Aware Guidance for World Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01559)
*Seungyeon Kim,Junhoo Lee,Baekseung Kim,Minkyu Kim,Nojun Kwak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练采样方法CAG（完成感知引导），解决世界动作模型（WAMs）在短块控制中出现"任务未完成想象"的失败问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界动作模型能预测视觉未来与机器人动作，但存在"任务未完成想象"问题：预测结果看似合理、与动作一致，却遗漏了完成任务所需的关键转变。作者指出这一失败并非源于世界模型主干本身，而是当模型被适配用于短块（short-chunk）控制时产生的，短块控制会反复偏向合理的局部延续，而非能完成任务的转变。

Method: 提出Completion Aware Guidance（CAG），一种免训练（training-free）的采样方法，在生成过程中引导模型朝向任务完成的方向进行采样。

Result: 在多个代表性WAM上：RoboTwin 2.0子集任务成功率从64%提升至70%；零样本仿真中从69%提升至75%；任务未完成想象的比例从79%降至40%。

Conclusion: 任务未完成想象的失败是可修正的采样问题而非模型骨架的固有限制，CAG作为一种免训练的引导方法能有效提升任务完成率并大幅减少不完整想象，具有较好的通用性与实用性。

Abstract: World Action Models (WAMs) predict visual futures and robot actions, yet they remain susceptible to task-incomplete imagination, where plausible, action-consistent predictions omit the transition needed for task completion. In this paper, we show that this failure is not inherent to the world model backbone, but emerges when adapted for short-chunk control, which can repeatedly favor plausible local continuations over task-completing transitions. To address this, we introduce Completion Aware Guidance (CAG), a training-free sampling method that guides generation toward task completion. Across representative WAMs, CAG improves success from 64% to 70% on a RoboTwin 2.0 subset and from 69% to 75% in zero-shot simulation, while reducing task-incomplete imagination from 79% to 40%.

</details>


### [233] [LiDARFlow: Real-Time Panel-Based MAV Guidance in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01573)
*João Machado,Zeynep Bilgin,Matthieu Verdoucq,Murat Bronz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅依赖机载感知的微型飞行器制导算法，基于气动势流理论的面板法生成光滑无碰撞制导向量，并通过在线构建障碍物表示扩展到未知环境，室内飞行实验验证了其可行性与轻量性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微型飞行器需要在未知、杂乱环境中仅依靠机载感知自主飞行，而现有方法在计算负担与机载实现上存在局限，因此需要一种轻量且实时的制导方案。

Method: 方法源于气动势流理论的面板公式，由局部感知到的障碍物生成光滑、无碰撞的制导向量；通过机载LiDAR测量在线构建并更新障碍物表示以扩展到未知环境；将所得避障场与标称制导向量场融合，生成最终控制输入。

Result: 在室内飞行测试的两个场景（航点导航与方向制导）中，飞行器均成功完成任务，并仅凭机载感知实时避开所有障碍物；结果表明该方法计算轻量、适合机载实现，点云处理是主要实际限制。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持在未知环境中实现轻量级机载制导的可行性。

Abstract: This paper presents a guidance algorithm for micro aerial vehicles operating in unknown, cluttered environments using only onboard sensing. The method is based on a panel formulation originally derived from aerodynamic potential-flow theory and generates smooth, collision-free guidance vectors from locally perceived obstacles. The approach is extended to unknown environments by constructing and updating the obstacle representation online from onboard LiDAR measurements. The resulting obstacle-avoidance field is integrated with a nominal guiding vector field to produce the final control input. The system is experimentally validated in indoor flight tests under two scenarios: waypoint navigation and directional guidance. In both cases, the vehicle successfully completes its task while avoiding all obstacles in real time using only onboard perception. The results demonstrate that the method is computationally lightweight and suitable for onboard implementation, with pointcloud processing identified as the main practical limitation. These results support the feasibility of lightweight onboard guidance in unknown environments.

</details>


### [234] [ReCo: Response-Consistent Locomotion with Policy-Aware MPC for Legged Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01612)
*Kuankuan Sima,Yichao Gao,Chenxi Gu,Kefan Zhao,Lin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ReCo框架将响应一致的行走策略与策略感知MPC相结合，实现连续腿足操作中精确的末端执行器跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续腿足操作要求末端执行器精准跟踪，同时基座持续行走。RL结合MPC适合此任务：学习策略提供鲁棒行走，MPC协调基座与机械臂补偿跟踪误差。但MPC只能补偿其可预测的基座运动，且学习策略的命令响应会随步态相位、接触和负载变化。

Method: 提出ReCo框架：通过响应塑形（response shaping）训练策略，使其在随机化动力学下对命令响应一致且可重复；再基于辨识得到的闭环响应模型，让MPC联合规划行走命令与机械臂运动。

Result: 在仿真基准上，ReCo相比各指标最优基线，将位置和姿态的均方根误差（RMSE）分别降低28.7%和27.4%。真实世界实验展示了机载的连续腿足操作，基座与机械臂运动协调。

Conclusion: 通过耦合响应一致的行走策略与策略感知MPC，ReCo有效提升了腿足操作的跟踪精度，并在仿真与真实实验中验证了其可行性。

Abstract: Continuous legged manipulation requires accurate end-effector tracking while the base keeps walking. Combining reinforcement learning (RL) with model predictive control (MPC) suits this task: the learned policy provides robust locomotion, while MPC coordinates the base and arm to compensate for tracking errors. However, MPC can compensate only for base motion that it can predict, and a learned policy's command response varies with gait phase, contact, and payload. We present ReCo, a framework that couples response-consistent locomotion with policy-aware MPC for legged manipulation. Response shaping trains the policy to respond to commands consistently and repeatably across randomized dynamics. An identified closed-loop response model then lets MPC jointly plan locomotion commands and arm motion. On the simulation benchmark, ReCo reduces position and orientation root-mean-square error (RMSE) by 28.7% and 27.4% relative to the best baseline for each metric. Real-world experiments demonstrate onboard continuous legged manipulation with coordinated base and arm motion.

</details>


### [235] [SonarVoxNet: Diver Detection in 3D Bounding Box using 3D Sonar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01644)
*Eugene Park,Jiwon Lee,Seyoung Kan,Trung Dong,Xiaomin Lin,Jane Shin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SonarVoxNet检测器与Diver3D数据集，实现仅用3D声纳对潜水员进行全9自由度三维检测，核心是yaw-only到全SO(3)旋转表示的转变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AUV协助潜水员需持续追踪其三维位置与全身姿态，但水下视觉感知不可靠；前视声纳丢弃高度信息，无法估计姿态，而商用3D声纳虽保留高度却返回稀疏噪声点，且现有检测器面向稠密LiDAR及仅绕yaw旋转的直立目标（车辆、行人），无法表示可自由俯仰翻滚的潜水员。

Method: 1) SonarVoxNet：将基于体素的编码器与无锚框的中心式检测头适配到3D声纳数据，用连续6D旋转参数化替代传统仅yaw旋转表示，预测完整9-DoF有向包围盒；2) Diver3D：首个公开的3D声纳数据集，包含自然洞穴潜水场景中多种非直立姿态潜水员的完整三维姿态标注；并对backbone与检测头进行受控消融实验。

Result: 消融实验表明，精确3D声纳潜水员检测的主导因素是旋转表示从yaw-only转为全SO(3)，该转变显著提升检测精度并降低姿态误差。

Conclusion: 仅凭3D声纳即可恢复潜水员全身姿态，为潜水员姿态估计与潜水员-机器人交互的未来研究奠定基础。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) assisting human divers must continuously track not only the diver's 3D position but also their full-body orientation. However, vision-based perception is unreliable underwater, and forward-looking sonar -- despite being widely used -- discards the elevation information needed for orientation estimation, posing a fundamental limitation. Recently commercialized 3D sonar preserves elevation but produces sparse, noisy returns, and existing detectors are built for dense LiDAR data and for targets that remain upright and rotate only about the yaw axis (e.g., vehicles, pedestrians), making them unable to represent a freely pitching and rolling diver. To address this gap, we present two contributions. First, SonarVoxNet adapts a voxel-based encoder and an anchor-free center-based detection head to 3D sonar data, replacing the conventional yaw-only rotation representation with a continuous 6D rotation parameterization to predict full 9-DoF oriented bounding boxes -- to our knowledge, the first 3D sonar diver detector to do so. Second, Diver3D is the first public 3D sonar dataset with full 3D orientation labels for divers in diverse, non-upright poses, collected at a natural cave-diving site. Through controlled ablations over the backbone and detection head, we show that the dominant factor behind accurate 3D sonar-based diver detection is the transition from yaw-only rotation to full-SO(3) rotation. This transition substantially improves detection accuracy and reduces orientation error. These results demonstrate that full-body diver orientation is recoverable from 3D sonar alone, laying the groundwork for future work on diver pose estimation and diver-robot interaction.

</details>


### [236] [Learning a Resolution-Consistent Jacobian Field for Bio-Inspired Rigid-Soft Finger](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01668)
*Tianyou Liang,Haisen Zeng,Shanjun Chen,YiMing Zhu,Zhongyue Lu,Zirong Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于条件流匹配的雅可比流匹配（JFM）框架，学习分辨率一致的雅可比场，提升腱驱动刚软耦合手指的建模与控制精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腱驱动刚软耦合灵巧手指具有强非线性和构型依赖灵敏度，传统基于雅可比的离散时间运动学算法依赖逐点局部线性近似，性能对传感器采样频率和控制器更新频率敏感，难以准确建模。

Method: 提出雅可比流匹配（JFM），一种基于条件流匹配（CFM）的结构化学习框架，将驱动到运动的转换建模为动力流，学习分辨率一致的雅可比场；支持单步预测和通过ODE积分的连续展开推理。

Result: 在腱驱动刚软手指上，单步预测精度提升，全局平均RMSE较基线离散雅可比学习降低超53%；长时程预测中，稀疏采样（Stride=8）下经ODE积分恢复的轨迹具有更高保真度，RMSE中位数降低14.43%，误差方差降低24.87%。

Conclusion: 所学习的基于流的雅可比场为刚软耦合非线性系统的离线多步轨迹优化提供了有效的局部模型。

Abstract: Bio-inspired tendon-driven rigid-soft coupled dexterous fingers exhibit strong nonlinearity and configuration-dependent sensitivity, making accurate modeling challenging. In discrete-time control, Jacobian-based kinematic algorithms typically rely on point-wise local linear approximations, which makes their performance sensitive to sensor sampling frequency and controller update frequency. To address this issue, we propose Jacobian Flow Matching (JFM), a structured learning framework based on Conditional Flow Matching (CFM), to learn a resolution-consistent Jacobian field that models actuation-to-motion transitions as a dynamical flow. The proposed framework supports both single-step prediction and continuous rollout via ODE integration, enabling consistent inference across temporal resolutions. Experiments on a tendon-driven rigid-soft finger show that the proposed method suppresses outlier errors and improves single-step prediction accuracy, reducing the global average RMSE by over 53% compared with a baseline discrete Jacobian learning approach. For long-horizon prediction, trajectories recovered via ODE integration achieve higher fidelity under sparse sampling (Stride = 8), reducing the RMSE median by 14.43% and the error variance by 24.87%. These results demonstrate that the learned flow-based Jacobian field provides an effective local model for offline multi-step trajectory optimization in rigid-soft coupled nonlinear systems.

</details>


### [237] [Beyond Leaderboard Scores: A Deployment-Focused Protocol for Interpretable Tracking Evaluation in Pedestrian-Centric Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01682)
*Dominik Wojcikiewicz,Diego Paez-Granados*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一套面向部署的"仅追踪器"评测协议，通过共享检测输入将追踪器行为与检测器质量解耦，直接评估初始化、检测间隙续接、身份恢复、近邻关联及运行时负载等能力，并在 JRDB 数据集上用六个开源追踪器和 PedRefTrack 进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人在行人中导航需要快速生成、空间可信、保持身份且适配嵌入式算力的轨迹。然而聚合型追踪指标（如单一 HOTA）难以揭示轨迹何时、如何失效，且检测器输入变化会混淆追踪器与检测器质量的归因，因此需要一套能隔离追踪器行为、揭示具体能力与失效模式的评测方法。

Method: 提出一种面向部署的"仅追踪器"评测协议，使用共享检测以隔离追踪器行为，直接评估初始化、检测器间隙续接、身份恢复、近邻关联以及负载相关的追踪步运行时；同时保留 HOTA 作为补充性聚合指标。在 JRDB 数据集上应用该协议，对比六个开源追踪器与轻量的 PedRefTrack，并加入 GT 辅助变体以估计理想关联与运动下的追踪器侧残余间隙。

Result: 在固定检测下，非 GT 追踪器的 HOTA 仅在 24.26%-29.67% 之间，但能力画像差异明显。检测器缺失 1.0 s 后，无 GT 辅助的追踪器在超过一半的合规案例中无法维持空间正确且身份一致；近邻失效较少，且主要在最短间距时增加。Jetson Orin 上追踪步运行时呈重尾且对负载敏感，导致若干追踪器在拥挤帧中低于 10 Hz 实时目标。

Conclusion: 检测缺失后的续接能力是所测属性中最主要的限制。该协议提供了一种可复现的方式来刻画以行人为中心环境中追踪器的行为与部署适用性，代码与评测脚本已公开。

Abstract: Mobile robots operating among pedestrians need trajectories that become available quickly, remain spatially credible through missed observations, preserve identity, and fit within an embedded computing budget. Aggregate tracking scores provide limited insight into when and how trajectories fail, while varying detector inputs can confound tracker and detector quality. We present a deployment-focused, tracker-only evaluation protocol that uses shared detections to isolate tracker behavior and directly evaluates initialization, detector-gap continuation, identity recovery, close-neighbor association, and load-dependent tracker-step runtime, while Higher Order Tracking Accuracy (HOTA) is retained as a complementary aggregate measure. We apply the protocol to the JackRabbot Dataset and Benchmark (JRDB) using six open-source trackers and our lightweight Pedestrian Reference Tracker (PedRefTrack), together with a GT-assisted variant that estimates the remaining tracker-side gap under idealized association and motion. Under fixed detections, the non-GT trackers span only 24.26%-29.67% HOTA yet exhibit markedly different capability profiles. After 1.0 s without detector support, no tracker without GT assistance maintains spatially correct, same-identity output in more than half of eligible cases, making missing-observation continuation the dominant limitation among the tested properties. Close-neighbor failures are smaller and increase mainly at the shortest separations. Tracker-step runtime on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin is heavy-tailed and load-sensitive, causing several trackers to fall below the 10 Hz real-time target in crowded frames. The protocol provides a reproducible way to characterize tracker behavior and deployment suitability in pedestrian-centric environments. Code and evaluation scripts are released at https://github.com/SCAI-Lab/tracker_eval.

</details>


### [238] [ActiveWAM: Evidence-Aware Active Vision for World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01698)
*Renjun Wu,Luzhou Ge,Xuesong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出 ActiveWAM，将主动视觉操作建模为"证据感知的保留-获取"问题，用统一的 world-action 模型联合学习观察与操作，并构建 RoboTwin-AV 基准，在多个任务上显著超越基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主动视觉操作需要策略同时控制相机与末端执行器，但相机的运动决定了有限观测窗口内哪些证据可见：获取新视角可能使任务关键线索移出视野，而保持视角又会错失有用观察。现有方法难以在这一取舍中有效决策。

Method: 作者将该问题形式化为证据感知的保留-获取（retain-acquire）问题，并提出 ActiveWAM——一个统一的世界-动作模型，联合学习观测与操作。核心是"训练时反演"（training-time inversion），用携带任务的源证据与可见时序变化约束冻结的视频先验，从而无需测试时反演或候选排序。部署时策略基于视角感知的历史生成双臂及 pan/tilt 动作（包括保持与重新获取行为），并从新测量的 RGB 观测更新上下文。未来视频预测作为协同训练信号，动作生成既不需要解码未来视频，也不需要最优视角标注。同时发布了 RoboTwin-AV，一个含可执行 pan/tilt 控制的 50 任务基准。

Result: ActiveWAM 在 TAVIS 分布外成功率上相比最强基线提升最高 17.0 个百分点；在 RoboTwin-AV 上比 Fast-WAM 高出 20.0 个百分点；在真实厨房物理任务上高出 26.7 个百分点。

Conclusion: 通过将相机运动决策与操作控制统一到一个世界-动作模型中，并以证据约束的训练时反演取代昂贵的测试时搜索，ActiveWAM 在仿真与真实任务上均取得显著提升，表明证据感知的视角决策对主动视觉操作至关重要。

Abstract: Active vision manipulation requires a policy to control both its camera and its end-effectors, yet camera motion determines which evidence remains visible within finite observation windows. Acquiring a new view can displace task-critical cues, while retaining a view forgoes potentially useful observations. We formulate this as an evidence-aware retain--acquire problem and present ActiveWAM, a unified world--action model that learns observation and manipulation jointly. To this end, we propose training-time inversion which constrains a frozen video prior by task-bearing source evidence and visible temporal changes, eliminating the need for test-time inversion or candidate ranking. At deployment, the policy generates bimanual and pan/tilt actions-including stay and reacquisition behaviors-from view-aware history, and updates context from newly measured RGB observations. Future-video prediction serves as a co-training signal, while action generation requires neither future-video decoding nor optimal viewpoint annotations. We introduce RoboTwin-AV, a 50-task benchmark with executable pan/tilt control and automatically generated demonstrations. ActiveWAM improves TAVIS out-of-distribution success by up to 17.0 percentage points over the strongest baselines, achieves 20.0 additional points over Fast-WAM on RoboTwin-AV, and outperforms it by 26.7 points on real-world physical kitchen tasks.

</details>


### [239] [Query-Conditioned Articulation Estimation from a Single Image](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01726)
*Abdelrhman Werby,Fabio Scaparro,Kai O. Arra*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: QueryArt 通过单张 RGB 图像、2D 查询点和相机内参估计铰接物体的运动学参数，避免了对部件分割的依赖，并在真实移动操作任务中取得 70.2% 成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作铰接物体需要估计其运动学参数，但通常只有单张 RGB 图像可用。现有单图方法将部件分割与铰接估计耦合，易受漏检和错误部件关联影响，且单视图只能恢复尺度不确定的度量 3D 几何，限制了实际应用。

Method: 提出 QueryArt 模型，输入单张 RGB 图像、一个 2D 查询点和相机内参。模型预测相对于查询点、以该点深度为单位的 3D 铰接几何，使其从图像本身可识别；再通过查询点的一次深度测量提供尺度，恢复度量参数。训练使用合成与真实铰接数据集的混合数据。

Result: 在多个基准上与近期基线对比，QueryArt 在大多数铰接指标上更优，包括分布外数据。在移动机械臂上进行了 57 次操作试验，覆盖 16 个物体部件和五类视角，成功率达 70.2%。

Conclusion: QueryArt 表明以查询点为中心、深度单位归一化的铰接参数表示能有效实现单图铰接估计，并在真实机器人操作中具备实用价值。

Abstract: Enabling robots to estimate the kinematic parameters of articulated objects unlocks a wide range of capabilities for interaction and manipulation. The estimation has to happen from the information the robot currently observes, often just a single RGB image of an object it has never seen before. Current single-image approaches couple articulation part segmentation with articulation estimation, making their predictions vulnerable to missed detections and incorrect part associations, and they regress metric 3D geometry that a single view fixes only up to scale. We present QueryArt, a model that estimates articulation parameters from a single RGB image, a 2D query point, and camera intrinsics. QueryArt is trained to estimate the 3D articulation geometry relative to the queried point and in units of its depth, which keeps its target identifiable from the image alone. A single depth measurement at the query point then supplies the scale and recovers the metric parameters. We train QueryArt on a curated mixture of synthetic and real-world articulation datasets. We evaluate QueryArt on several benchmarks and compare it against recent baselines. QueryArt outperforms recent baselines on most articulation metrics, including on out-of-distribution data. To demonstrate the model's capabilities in real-world settings, we evaluate QueryArt on a mobile manipulator across 57 manipulation trials spanning 16 object parts and five viewpoint classes, achieving a 70.2% success rate. We provide code and videos at: https://abwerby.github.io/queryart/

</details>


### [240] [World Motion Models: Flexible Sequence Modeling of SE(3) Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01742)
*Jiahui Lei,Qianqian Wang,Trevor Darrell,Angjoo Kanazawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出World Motion Models（WMMs），用稀疏SE(3)位姿轨迹统一建模动态3D世界，将多种任务转化为同一网络上的不同掩码应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 赋予人工代理空间智能需要关于动态3D世界的全面生成先验。现有方法缺乏一个统一、表达力强的4D建模表示，难以覆盖关节物体、人体、手物交互、场景动态、相机运动乃至机器人状态与动作等多种实体。

Method: 基于动态场景元素可由一组刚性SE(3)轨迹良好近似的观察，用稀疏SE(3)位姿轨迹作为4D建模的最小且具表达力的基元，把上述多类实体统一到共享空间。将该联合分布建模为灵活的序列建模问题，采用带逐token噪声水平的flow-matching，并引入context token机制处理非序列条件，从而支持任意数量实体与时间步之间的any-to-any边际条件建模。不同任务通过对同一网络施加不同掩码来实现。

Result: 在3D视觉与机器人学的6个多样应用中，WMMs展现出良好的通用性与灵活性，并取得强劲性能。

Conclusion: WMMs通过统一的SE(3)轨迹表示与掩码化序列建模框架，将未来预测、运动插补、模型预测控制、逆运动学、跨体现重定向、策略学习等任务统一起来，验证了该表示作为动态3D世界生成先验的有效性。

Abstract: Equipping artificial agents with spatial intelligence requires a comprehensive generative prior over the dynamic 3D world. We propose World Motion Models (WMMs) that capture "what was, is, and will be where across time" via sparse SE(3) pose trajectories. WMMs are built on the observation that elements of dynamic scenes can be well approximated by a set of rigid SE(3) trajectories, a minimal yet expressive primitive for 4D modeling. This representation unifies articulated objects, human bodies, hand-object interactions, piecewise-rigid scene dynamics, camera motion, and even robot states and actions into a single shared space. Given this representation, we cast the joint distribution of these entities as a flexible sequence modeling problem, utilizing flow-matching with per-token noise levels. Coupled with a context token mechanism for non-sequential conditioning, this formulation supports any-to-any marginal conditioning across an arbitrary number of entities and time steps. Tasks such as future prediction, motion infilling, model-predictive control, inverse kinematics, cross-embodiment retargeting, and policy learning all reduce to the application of different masks over the same network. Experiments on 6 diverse applications of 3D vision and robotics demonstrate the versatility and flexibility of WMMs with strong performance.

</details>


### [241] [End-to-End Learning vs. Modular Architectures: Comparative Insights into Autonomous Driving Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01746)
*Kartik B. Kapse*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文对自动驾驶的端到端学习与模块化架构进行对比分析，并提出四维架构选择框架，用于指导不同应用场景下的架构选型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶是智能交通研究的核心，端到端学习与模块化架构是两种主流设计范式，但缺乏对其优劣势、权衡关系及应用适配性的系统性对比，因此需要综合梳理以指导实践与后续研究。

Method: 采用文献综合与比较分析方法，围绕可解释性、可扩展性、鲁棒性和实际适用性等关键因素对两种范式进行评估；并在此基础上提出四维架构选择框架，包含安全、运行环境、数据/计算资源、部署情境四个维度共十二项二值准则。

Result: 对比显示端到端学习强调简洁与动态环境适应性，但缺乏透明性且高度依赖大规模数据；模块化架构可解释性强、便于任务级优化，但集成复杂、可扩展性受限。所提出的选择框架在十个已发表的自动驾驶系统上验证，正确推荐了7/10的部署架构。

Conclusion: 两种范式各有明显权衡，混合架构结合双方优势，是克服各自局限的可行方向，也是未来研究的重要方向。

Abstract: Autonomous driving systems have become a central focus of intelligent transportation research, with End-to-End Learning and Modular Architectures offering two prominent design paradigms for their implementation. E2E Learning uses deep learning algorithms to map raw sensory inputs directly to driving actuators, providing a streamlined and adaptable solution. while Modular Architectures employ a pipeline-based approach, dividing the system into distinct subsystems for perception, cognition, planning, and control. This paper presents a comprehensive comparative analysis of these paradigms, focusing on their strengths, limitations, and trade-offs to provide insights into their suitability for various autonomous driving applications. The study evaluates key factors such as interpretability, scalability, robustness, and real-world applicability. While End-to-End Learning emphasizes simplicity and adaptability in dynamic environments, it lacks transparency and is highly dependent on large datasets. Conversely, Modular Architectures offer superior interpretability and task-specific optimization, but face challenges related to integration complexity and scalability. To address these limitations, hybrid approaches that combine the strengths of both paradigms have emerged, offering a promising direction for overcoming these challenges. Beyond this comparative synthesis, following work proposes a Four-Dimensional Architecture Selection Framework, comprising twelve binary criteria across safety, operating environment, data/computational resources, and deployment context, and validate it against ten published autonomous driving systems, correctly recommending 7/10 deployed architectures. This work synthesizes existing literature to highlight key trade-offs between the paradigms and identifies hybrid architectures as a promising direction for future research.

</details>


### [242] [FlashDexRetarget: Accelerating Dexterous Manipulation Data Generation through Multi-Motion Retargeting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01849)
*Kyungmin Lee,Sibeen Kim,Dongyoon Hwang,Yoonsang Oh,Donghu Kim,Youngdo Lee,I Made Aswin Nahrendra,Jaegul Choo,Hojoon Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FlashDexRetarget，基于强化学习的高成功率、高效率灵巧手动作重定向框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类手-物演示是可复用的灵巧操作数据来源，但跨本体迁移需要物理可行的重定向。现有基于物理的方法在重定向成功率、动作特定训练效率，或二者上存在局限。

Method: 结合物体点云观测、手-物距离特征与未来轨迹编码，并配合监督物体运动与参考手-物关系的互补奖励；采用左右手分离的actor-critic网络，并将离线策略算法FlashSAC适配到灵巧动作跟踪。

Result: 在涵盖单物体与双物体交互的50个动作基准上达到90%成功率，约为所评估采样类基线的2.5倍，训练算力比所评估RL基线最多少100倍。在XHand与Sharpa Wave Hand上均取得一致提升，消融实验验证各设计贡献。200、500、1000个动作的更大规模实验表明方法在大规模下保持稳定且随训练集增大更高效。真实世界采集演示的回放结果展示了框架适用性。

Conclusion: FlashDexRetarget实现了高成功率、高效率且可扩展的灵巧动作重定向，适用于真实人类操作记录。

Abstract: Human hand-object demonstrations offer a reusable source of dexterous robot manipulation data, but transferring them across embodiments requires physically feasible retargeting. Existing physics-based approaches face limitations in retargeting success, motion-specific training efficiency, or both. To address these limitations, we introduce FlashDexRetarget, an RL-based framework for high-success, efficient dexterous motion retargeting. To make the demonstrated interaction easier to learn, we combine object point-cloud observations, hand-object distance features, and future trajectory encodings with complementary rewards that supervise object motion and reference hand-object relationships. To further accelerate learning, we employ separate left- and right-hand actor critic networks and adapt the off-policy algorithm, FlashSAC to dexterous motion tracking. On a benchmark of 50 motions spanning single-object and two-object interactions, FlashDexRetarget achieves a 90% success rate, approximately 2.5x that of the evaluated sampling-based baselines, while requiring up to 100x less training compute than the evaluated RL-based baselines. Evaluations on both XHand and Sharpa Wave Hand show consistent gains, and component-wise ablations examine the contributions of our design choices. Beyond the 50-motion benchmark, experiments with 200, 500, and 1,000 motions demonstrate that our method remains stable at larger scales and produces successful retargeted motions more efficiently as the training set grows. Qualitative replay results using real-world-captured demonstrations further illustrate the applicability of our framework to recorded human manipulation. Videos and code are available at https://davian-robotics.github.io/FlashDexRetarget/

</details>


### [243] [ChunkVLA-AM: Parallel Action Chunking for Vision-Language-Action Robot Control in Additive Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01856)
*Zhugang Liu,Kaichuang Zhang,Jinman Zhang,Pu Sun,Martha Asare,Jose Hernandez,Maxim Ermolinsky,Efren Saenz,Qi Lu,Jinghao Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将 OpenVLA-OFT 部署到 FAIRINO FR3 机器人固定增材制造工作单元的框架，通过数据管线实现具身适配，并采用云边架构完成闭环执行，42次物体转移试验成功39次（92.9%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型为增材制造自动化带来新机遇，但将其适配到未见过的机器人具身成本高昂，且环境变化会导致性能退化，因此需要可行的部署方案。

Method: 构建数据管线，将单目真实世界演示转换为 OpenVLA 兼容的 TFDS/RLDS 数据集以适配 FR3 具身；运行时每次推理预测8步的7维动作块，FR3 开环执行每个动作块后再采集新观测，实现块间闭环反馈；采用云边架构，FR3 客户端通过 FastAPI 接口将观测流式传输到远程推理服务器。

Result: 在42次物理 A 到 B 物体转移试验中（红蓝目标各半），成功39次，成功率92.9%；3次失败均发生在最终放置阶段，因释放高度控制不足导致物体倾倒。光照扫描发现 0-255 尺度下 85-125 为低误差亮度区间，亮度95时平均空间误差最低。

Conclusion: 该框架验证了 OpenVLA-OFT 在增材制造固定工作单元中部署的可行性，成功率较高，但释放高度控制和光照条件仍是影响性能的关键因素。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models unify visual perception, language understanding, and action generation, offering new opportunities for automation in additive manufacturing (AM). However, deployment in AM remains challenging because adapting these models to unseen robot embodiments is costly, and performance can degrade under environment changes. In this work, we present a framework for deploying OpenVLA-OFT on a FAIRINO FR3 robot in a fixed AM workcell. A data pipeline converts monocular real-world demonstrations into OpenVLA-compatible TFDS/RLDS datasets to support adaptation to the FR3 embodiment. At runtime, each inference request predicts an eight-step chunk of 7-D actions. The FR3 executes each chunk open loop before capturing a new observation, providing closed-loop feedback between chunks. The system uses a cloud-edge architecture in which the FR3 client streams observations to a remote inference server through a FastAPI interface. In 42 physical A-to-B object-transfer trials, evenly split between red and blue targets, the system succeeded in 39 (92.9%). All three failures occurred during final placement, when insufficient release-height control caused the object to topple. An illumination sweep identified a low-error luminance range of 85-125 on a 0-255 scale, with the lowest mean spatial error at 95.

</details>


### [244] [Towards Physical Underwater Robotic Assistance for Scuba Diver Movement in Confined Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01906)
*Demetrious T. Kutzke,Junaed Sattar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出首个可穿戴机器人RADMCS，通过推进器触觉反馈帮助潜水员在受限空间中保持与水下结构的固定距离，并完成8名潜水员的IRB水下实验验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潜水员通常被训练控制深度以避免快速升降造成的伤害，但许多水下任务（如珊瑚礁、水下钻探仪器或未爆炸物等海底结构附近作业）需要横向距离控制。现有推进辅助外骨骼主要提供推进助力，缺乏针对方向引导的解决方案。

Method: 提出RADMCS可穿戴机器人，利用单目深度估计感知技术，结合潜水推进器的力反馈，向潜水员提供触觉方向引导。设备体积小、结构紧凑，可作为可扩展的基础平台。开展IRB批准的水下研究，8名潜水员参与，涵盖闭水泳池与海洋环境中的阈值敏感性测试、闭水环境中的距离保持实验，以及海洋中的形态、贴合与功能测试。

Result: 实验表明，相对较低的推力值（最大值的10%）即可通过机器人的物理引导感觉实现对人类运动方向的控制，并成功验证距离保持能力。

Conclusion: RADMCS证明了以触觉力反馈方式进行潜水员方向引导的可行性，为后续更复杂的控制与导航行为提供了基础平台。

Abstract: Scuba divers are taught to control their depth to avoid rapid ascents and descents, which could result in serious injuries such as gas embolisms and barotrauma. However, many underwater tasks necessitate lateral control, maintaining distance between subsea structures such as coral reefs, submerged drilling instrumentation, or unexploded ordnance. In this work, we discuss a first-of-its-kind wearable robotic solution providing thruster-actuated directional guidance to a diver, as distinct from prior propulsive-assistance exoskeletons. We introduce ``Robotic Assisted Diver Movement in Confined Spaces'' (RADMCS), a wearable robot that assists divers in maintaining a fixed distance from subsea structures by leveraging perception techniques in monocular depth estimation and force-feedback from submersible thrusters to provide haptic feedback. Its small and compact form factor creates a foundational platform that could be expanded to include more sophisticated control and navigation behaviors. We present results from Institutional Review Board (IRB) in-water studies with eight human scuba diver participants on threshold sensitivity tests in both a closed-water swimming facility and ocean environments; distance-maintaining experiments in a closed-water facility; and form, fit, and function testing in the ocean. We demonstrate that relatively low thrust values (10 percent of maximum) allow robotic direction of a human's movement using the physical sensation of the robot's guidance.

</details>


### [245] [Robot Learning on Discrete Surfaces: Theory and Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.01910)
*Matteo Dalle Vedove,Fares J. Abu-Dakka,Luigi Palopoli,Daniele Fontanelli,Matteo Saveriano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出统一的离散黎曼框架，让机器人学习可直接在多面体表面网格上进行，并在DMP、GP、流匹配三种范式上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界中的物体都被表面包围，但多数机器人学习与运动生成框架仅将表面视为约束，忽略了其内在几何；尤其对于CAD和三维重建常见的多面体网格，其离散几何结构未被充分利用。

Method: 基于离散微分几何，定义在面、边、顶点间均良定义的指数/对数映射、平行移动和环境空间投影；并实例化到三种学习范式：(i) DMP，采用改进的指数映射计算、固定切锥强迫项编码与平行移动；(ii) GP，使用带可容许性控制的测地核；(iii) 黎曼流匹配，使用网格原生算子。

Result: 仿真中与最先进方法相比，DMP取得更好的跨表面泛化与稳定性；GP无需光滑性假设即可在网格任意位置回归；流匹配生成质量优于谱基线且训练时间更短。两个真实机器人场景验证成功：跨不同表面泛化用户绘制轨迹，以及在RGB-D重建表面上规划抛光运动。

Conclusion: 该离散黎曼框架可统一支持多面体网格上的机器人学习，兼顾理论良定义性与实际任务表现。

Abstract: All the objects composing our world are enclosed within surfaces. Yet, most robot learning and motion generation frameworks treat surfaces as constraints ignoring their intrinsic geometry. This gap is acute for polyhedral meshes--the standard output of CAD and 3D reconstruction--whose discrete geometric structure remains unexploited. In this paper, we propose a unified discrete Riemannian framework that enables robot learning directly on polyhedral surface meshes.
